<tr><td colspan="7">例 They won the contract through underhanded tactics.  他们靠不正当手段赢得了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aboveboard</td><td>/əˈbʌvbɔːrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无欺诈的，光明正大的</td><td>open and aboveboard<br>公开而光明正大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole deal was entirely open and aboveboard.  整笔交易完全是光明正大的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 honest</td><td>/ˈɑːnəst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诚实的，正直的</td><td>an honest answer<br>一个诚实的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me an honest answer, even if it disappoints me.  给我一个诚实的答复，即使会让我失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straight</td><td>/ˈstreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直的，笔直的</td><td>a straight road<br>笔直的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drive straight ahead until you reach the second traffic light.  一直往前开，直到第二个红绿灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dwindle</b></td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decrease</td><td>/dɪˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减少，下降</td><td>a sharp decrease<br>急剧下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic accidents decreased noticeably after the new speed limits appeared.  新限速出台后交通事故明显减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drop</td><td>/ˈdrɑːp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，减少；落下</td><td>drop sharply<br>急剧下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales dropped by half after the price increase.  涨价后销量下降了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reduce</td><td>/rəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减少，降低</td><td>reduce the cost sharply<br>大幅降低成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New machines reduced the factory's energy use by half.  新机器把工厂的能耗降低了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrink</td><td>/ʃrɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（在数量或价值方面）降低，减小</td><td>shrink in value<br>价值缩水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The value of the old coins began to shrink after the scandal.  丑闻之后，那些旧币的价值开始缩水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taper</td><td>/ˈteɪpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>逐渐减少，减弱</td><td>taper off gradually<br>逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm tapered off gradually as the evening wore on.  随着夜色渐深，暴风雨逐渐平息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knock down</td><td>/ˈnɑk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拆卸；击倒</td><td>knock down the shed<br>拆掉棚屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They knocked down the old barn and sold the timber.  他们拆掉了旧谷仓并卖掉了木料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balloon</td><td>/bəˈluːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速增加</td><td>costs have ballooned<br>成本迅速膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repair costs ballooned from two thousand to twenty thousand dollars.  维修费用从两千美元猛增到两万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 burgeon</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迅速成长扩大，蓬勃发展</td><td>a burgeoning market<br>迅速发展的市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's tech sector burgeoned after the new university opened.  新大学开办后，该市的科技产业迅速壮大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 increase</td><td>/ɪnˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>增加，增长</td><td>increase production sharply<br>大幅提高产量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales increased steadily after the store extended its opening hours.  延长营业时间后销量稳步增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dyspeptic</b></td><td>/dɪsˈpeptɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的</td><td>a dyspeptic old man<br>一个脾气坏的老头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dyspeptic old man shouted at the children for stepping on his lawn.  一个脾气坏的老头因孩子踩了他的草坪而大声呵斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cantankerous</td><td>/kænˈtæŋkərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，爱争吵的</td><td>a cantankerous neighbor<br>一个爱吵架的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their cantankerous landlord argued about every small repair.  他们那位爱争吵的房东为每项小修理都要吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagreeable</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈɡriːəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人不快的；脾气坏的</td><td>a disagreeable task<br>令人不快的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cleaning the drains was a thoroughly disagreeable job.  清理下水道是件极其讨人厌的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irritable</td><td>/ˈɪrətəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，急躁的</td><td>become irritable and tense<br>变得易怒紧张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lack of sleep makes him irritable by mid-afternoon.  睡眠不足使他到下午中段就变得易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fretful</td><td>/ˈfretfl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，烦躁的</td><td>a fretful child<br>烦躁的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The baby was fretful all night because of the heat.  因为炎热，婴儿整夜烦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irascible</td><td>/ɪˈræsəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>an irascible old man<br>脾气暴躁的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The irascible coach shouted at the referee over every call.  这位暴躁的教练对每一次判罚都冲裁判大喊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peevish</td><td>/ˈpiːvɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，爱抱怨的</td><td>a peevish complaint<br>不耐烦的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered in a peevish tone and refused to explain further.  她用不耐烦的语气回答，拒绝再解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 splenetic</del></td><td>/splɪˈnetɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，脾气坏的</td><td>a splenetic outburst<br>暴躁的发作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a splenetic review of the new opera.  这位批评家为新歌剧写了一篇火气十足的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surly</td><td>/ˈsɜːrli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气不好的</td><td>a surly waiter<br>一个态度恶劣的侍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surly waiter took our order without once looking up.  一个态度恶劣的侍者点单时始终没抬头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amiable</td><td>/ˈeɪmiəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好脾气的，友好易相处的</td><td>an amiable manner<br>和气友善的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our amiable guide answered the same question a dozen times.  我们和气的导游把同一个问题回答了十几遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easygoing</td><td>/ˌiːziˈɡoʊɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>随和的，脾气好的</td><td>an easygoing manner<br>随和的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her easygoing nature makes her popular with colleagues.  她随和的性格使她受同事欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 good-humored</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈhjumərd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气好的，和气的</td><td>a good-humored laugh<br>和气的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed good-humored even after losing the final match.  即使输掉决赛，他仍然保持好脾气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 good-natured</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈneɪtʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气好的，和善的</td><td>good-natured teasing<br>善意的调侃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He accepted the joke with a good-natured shrug.  他和善地耸耸肩接受了这个玩笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 good-tempered</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>性情温和的，不易发怒的</td><td>a good-tempered child<br>脾气温和的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old dog was good-tempered even when children pulled its ears.  即使孩子们拉它的耳朵，这只老狗也很温顺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>earnest</b></td><td>/ˈɜːrnɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃认真的</td><td>an earnest student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An earnest student stayed behind to ask about the summer reading list.  一名认真的学生留下来询问暑期阅读书目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grave</td><td>/ˈɡreɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>严肃的，严重的；坟墓</td><td>a grave expression<br>严肃的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor's grave tone told us the news was bad.  医生严肃的语气告诉我们消息不好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 serious</td><td>/ˈsɪriəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的，认真的；严重的</td><td>a serious student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is serious about becoming a research scientist.  她认真地想成为一名研究科学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solemn</td><td>/ˈsɑːləm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>庄严的，郑重的</td><td>a solemn promise<br>郑重的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony was solemn and lasted almost two hours.  仪式庄严肃穆，持续了近两个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 staid</td><td>/ˈsteɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>拘谨的，古板严肃的</td><td>a staid old bank<br>古板的老银行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The staid law firm finally allowed casual clothing.  这家古板的律所终于允许穿便装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facetious</td><td>/fəˈsiːʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜欢开玩笑的，轻浮的</td><td>a facetious remark<br>轻浮的玩笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His facetious remark at the funeral offended nearly everyone present.  他在葬礼上轻浮的玩笑话得罪了几乎所有在场的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frivolous</td><td>/ˈfrɪvələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻浮的；无关紧要的</td><td>a frivolous remark<br>轻率无谓的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the frivolous lawsuit within a single morning.  法官在一个上午之内就驳回了这起无理的诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flip</td><td>/ˈflɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>轻率的，不恭的；轻抛</td><td>a flip answer<br>轻率的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flip reply annoyed the interviewer.  他轻率的回答让面试官不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flippant</td><td>/ˈflɪpənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻率的，不严肃的</td><td>a flippant tone<br>轻率的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She regretted her flippant comment about the accident.  她后悔自己对那场事故的轻率评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 playful</td><td>/ˈpleɪfl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱开玩笑的，好打闹的</td><td>a playful tone<br>玩闹的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her playful tone made it clear that she was only teasing him.  她玩闹的语气清楚表明她只是在逗他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embryonic</b></td><td>/ˌembriˈɑːnɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>萌芽期的；胚胎的</td><td>at an embryonic stage<br>处于萌芽阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project is still embryonic, so details may change considerably.  该项目仍处于萌芽阶段，细节可能会有很大变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 aborning</del></td><td>/əˈbɔrnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>刚萌芽的，正在诞生中的</td><td>die aborning<br>尚未成形便夭折</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform movement died aborning when funding vanished.  资金消失后，改革运动尚未成形就夭折了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beginning</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>最初的，开端的；开始</td><td>the beginning stages<br>起始阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are still at the beginning of the investigation.  我们仍处于调查的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inceptive</del></td><td>/ɪnˈseptɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>开始的，起始的</td><td>an inceptive phase<br>起始阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project is still in its inceptive stage.  该项目仍处于起始阶段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incipient</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪpiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>起初的，初现的</td><td>incipient signs of decay<br>初现的衰败迹象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist spotted incipient signs of decay in two of the teeth.  牙医发现两颗牙齿上有初现的蛀坏迹象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inchoate</td><td>/ɪnˈkoʊət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>新生的，才开始的</td><td>inchoate ideas<br>尚未成形的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inchoate plans changed every time he described them to us.  他那些尚未成形的计划每次向我们描述时都变了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 initial</td><td>/ˌɪˈnɪʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>最初的，开始的</td><td>the initial reaction<br>最初的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our initial estimate turned out to be far too low.  我们最初的估计结果远远偏低。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nascent</td><td>/ˈnæsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新生的</td><td>a nascent industry<br>新兴的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Government loans helped the nascent solar industry survive its first decade.  政府贷款帮助新兴的太阳能产业度过了最初十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rudimentary</td><td>/ˌruːdɪˈmentri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>初始的，未发展的；最根本的，基础的</td><td>rudimentary knowledge<br>基础的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had only a rudimentary knowledge of chemistry when he joined the lab.  他加入实验室时对化学只有基础的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adult</td><td>/əˈdʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>成年的，成熟的；成年人</td><td>an adult attitude<br>成熟的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the crisis in a calm, adult way.  她以冷静成熟的方式处理了这场危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full-blown</td><td>/ˌfʊlˈbloʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全成熟的，全面爆发的</td><td>a full-blown crisis<br>全面爆发的危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor dispute grew into a full-blown lawsuit.  一场小争执演变成全面的诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full-fledged</td><td>/ˌfʊl ˈfledʒd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全成熟的，羽翼已成的</td><td>a full-fledged member<br>正式成员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three years she became a full-fledged engineer.  三年后她成为一名完全合格的工程师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mature</td><td>/məˈtʃʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>成熟的；（使）成熟</td><td>a mature judgment<br>成熟的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seems remarkably mature for a boy of fifteen.  对一个十五岁男孩来说他显得格外成熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ripe</td><td>/ˈraɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>成熟的，熟透的；时机成熟的</td><td>ripe for change<br>变革时机成熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaches are finally ripe enough to pick and eat.  这些桃子终于熟到可以采下来吃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>earsplitting</b></del></td><td>/ˈɪrˌsplɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳欲聋的</td><td>an earsplitting shriek<br>震耳的尖叫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An earsplitting shriek from the whistle stopped every conversation on the platform.  哨子发出的震耳尖啸让站台上所有的交谈都停了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blaring</td><td>/ˈblerɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>响亮刺耳的，轰鸣的</td><td>blaring sirens<br>刺耳的警笛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A blaring horn woke everyone in the street.  刺耳的喇叭声惊醒了街上所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blasting</td><td>/ˈblæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳的，轰响的</td><td>blasting music<br>震耳的音乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blasting music from the next flat kept me awake.  隔壁公寓震耳的音乐让我无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deafening</td><td>/ˈdefənɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>震耳欲聋的</td><td>deafening applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd's cheer was deafening as the winner appeared.  获胜者出现时人群的欢呼震耳欲聋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loud</td><td>/ˈlaʊd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>大声的，响亮的</td><td>a loud bang<br>一声巨响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The music was too loud for normal conversation.  音乐太响，无法正常交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 piercing</td><td>/ˈpɪrsɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖利刺耳的；刺骨的</td><td>a piercing scream<br>尖利的尖叫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A piercing whistle stopped the game immediately.  一声尖利的哨响立刻中止了比赛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plangent</del></td><td>/ˈplændʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）响亮而哀伤的</td><td>a plangent cry<br>哀伤的呼声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plangent cry of a gull echoed across the empty beach.  海鸥凄凉的叫声在空荡的海滩上回响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resounding</td><td>/rɪˈzaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>响亮的，回响的；巨大的</td><td>a resounding crash<br>响亮的撞击声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The door shut with a resounding bang behind him.  门在他身后砰然作响地关上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roaring</td><td>/ˈrɔːrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>咆哮的，轰鸣的</td><td>a roaring engine<br>轰鸣的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We could hardly talk over the roaring waterfall.  在轰鸣的瀑布声中我们几乎无法交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stentorian</td><td>/stenˈtɔːriən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音洪亮的</td><td>a stentorian voice<br>洪亮的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stentorian voice carried easily to the back of the hall.  他洪亮的嗓音轻松传到大厅后排。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thunderous</td><td>/ˈθʌndərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>雷鸣般的，轰响的</td><td>thunderous applause<br>雷鸣般的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The performance ended in thunderous applause.  演出在雷鸣般的掌声中结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gentle</td><td>/ˈdʒentəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，轻柔的</td><td>a gentle voice<br>温柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the door a gentle push and stepped inside.  他轻轻推了一下门，走了进去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soft</td><td>/ˈsɑːft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔和的，轻微的</td><td>a soft voice<br>轻柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a soft tone so the baby would not wake.  她轻声说话，以免把婴儿弄醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>eavesdrop</b></td><td>/ˈiːvzdrɑːp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>偷听</td><td>eavesdrop on a conversation<br>偷听谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was caught trying to eavesdrop on a conversation in the next room.  她被抓到试图偷听隔壁房间的谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overhear</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈhɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>无意中听到，偶然听见</td><td>overhear a private remark<br>无意听到私下的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I happened to overhear two colleagues discussing the layoffs in the hallway.  我在走廊上偶然听到两位同事在谈裁员的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wiretap</td><td>/ˈwaɪərtæp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>偷听，窃听</td><td>wiretap a suspect's phone<br>窃听嫌疑人电话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Agents obtained a court order to wiretap the suspect's office phone.  探员取得法院许可，窃听嫌疑人办公室的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ebullient</b></td><td>/ɪˈbʌliənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情洋溢的，兴高采烈的</td><td>an ebullient host<br>热情洋溢的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An ebullient host greeted every guest at the door with genuine delight.  热情洋溢的主人在门口由衷欢喜地迎接每位客人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boiling</td><td>/ˈbɔɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沸腾的；情绪激昂的</td><td>a boiling pot of water<br>一锅沸水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dropped the noodles into the boiling water and stirred once.  她把面条下进沸水里，搅了一下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exuberant</td><td>/ɪɡˈzuːbərənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情洋溢的；繁茂的</td><td>exuberant applause from fans<br>粉丝热情的掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Exuberant applause from fans greeted the singer's surprise return.  粉丝们热情洋溢的掌声迎接歌手的意外回归。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effervescent</td><td>/ˌefərˈvesənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情活泼的；起泡的</td><td>an effervescent personality<br>活泼热情的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her effervescent laughter filled the small crowded kitchen.  她欢快的笑声充满了狭小拥挤的厨房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vivacious</td><td>/vɪˈveɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼的，快活的</td><td>a vivacious personality<br>活泼的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her vivacious manner made every stranger feel welcome at the table.  她活泼的举止让每个陌生人在餐桌旁都感到受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tepid</td><td>/ˈtepɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不太热心的</td><td>a tepid response<br>不热情的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal met a tepid response from the board members.  提案得到董事们不热情的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torpid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的，无生气的</td><td>a torpid winter afternoon<br>慵懒迟滞的冬日下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left the whole village torpid and unwilling to move.  酷热让整个村子都变得麻木迟钝，不愿动弹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>eccentric</b></td><td>/ɪkˈsentrɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>古怪的，不循规蹈矩的</td><td>an eccentric old professor<br>行为古怪的老教授</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An eccentric old professor kept three clocks that all showed different times.  一位行为古怪的老教授留着三只指着不同时间的钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strange</td><td>/ˈstreɪndʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；陌生的</td><td>a strange noise upstairs<br>楼上奇怪的声响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a strange smell coming from the kitchen last night.  昨晚厨房里传出一股奇怪的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bizarre</td><td>/bəˈzɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怪异的，离奇的</td><td>a bizarre coincidence<br>离奇的巧合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By some bizarre coincidence we wore the same coat.  出于某种离奇的巧合，我们穿了同款外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erratic</td><td>/ɪˈrætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，不规律的</td><td>erratic driving behavior<br>飘忽不定的驾驶行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His erratic driving behavior alarmed the passengers long before the police stopped him.  早在警察拦下他之前，他飘忽不定的驾驶行为就让乘客惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 idiosyncratic</td><td>/ˌɪdioʊsɪnˈkrætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>特有的，怪癖的</td><td>an idiosyncratic writing style<br>极具个人特色的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His idiosyncratic habit of numbering every page twice confused the editors.  他给每页编两次号的怪癖让编辑们很困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 odd</td><td>/ˈɑːd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；奇数的</td><td>an odd pair of socks<br>不成对的袜子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed odd that nobody answered the door at noon.  中午没人应门，这显得有点奇怪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offbeat</td><td>/ˌɔːfˈbiːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平常的</td><td>an offbeat sense of humor<br>别致的幽默感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His offbeat sense of humor won over an audience that had arrived skeptical.  他别致的幽默感赢得了原本抱着怀疑到场的观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outlandish</td><td>/aʊtˈlændɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古怪的，奇异的</td><td>outlandish conspiracy theories<br>离奇的阴谋论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her outlandish hat made every head turn as she entered the hall.  她走进大厅时，那顶古怪的帽子让所有人回头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quaint</td><td>/ˈkweɪnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古雅有趣的；离奇的</td><td>a quaint fishing village<br>古朴别致的渔村</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stayed in a quaint cottage with a thatched roof and stone walls.  我们住在一间茅草顶石墙的古雅小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weird</td><td>/ˈwɪrd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怪异的，离奇的</td><td>a weird coincidence<br>离奇的巧合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film has a weird ending that nobody in the theater understood.  这部电影的结尾很怪，影院里没人看懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fame</td><td>/ˈfeɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>名声，声誉</td><td>rise to sudden fame<br>骤然成名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fame spread quickly after the documentary aired nationwide.  纪录片在全国播出后，她的名声迅速传开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renown</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>声望，名誉</td><td>a chef of great renown<br>声望极高的厨师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violinist won international renown while still in her twenties.  这位小提琴家二十多岁就赢得了国际声望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brilliance</td><td>/ˈbrɪljəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>才华出众；光辉灿烂</td><td>the brilliance of his argument<br>他论证的精彩之处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics praised the brilliance of the young director's first film.  批评家盛赞这位年轻导演处女作的精彩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 triumph</td><td>/ˈtraɪəmf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>巨大成功，胜利</td><td>a stunning military triumph<br>惊人的军事胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new bridge is a triumph of modern engineering and careful planning.  这座新桥是现代工程与周密规划的巨大成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ordinary</td><td>/ˈɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普通的，平常的</td><td>just an ordinary weekday<br>只是平常的工作日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked like an ordinary commuter waiting for the morning bus.  他看上去就像个等早班公交的普通上班族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regular</td><td>/ˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>规律的，通常的</td><td>on a regular basis<br>定期地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He goes swimming on a regular basis every week.  他每周都定期去游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debacle</td><td>/deɪˈbɑːkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>溃败</td><td>an economic debacle<br>经济上的溃败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign ended in a debacle that cost the party three seats.  这场竞选以溃败收场，使该党丢掉三个席位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fiasco</td><td>/fiˈæskoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大失败</td><td>end in a fiasco<br>以惨败告终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poorly planned launch party ended in a complete fiasco.  筹划糟糕的发布会以彻底的失败告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>eclipse</b></td><td>/ɪˈklɪps/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使声望下降，使黯然失色</td><td>eclipse his earlier fame<br>使他早年的名声黯然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His later failures eclipsed the fame he had earned as a young pianist.  他后来的失败使他年轻时作为钢琴家赢得的名声黯然失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adumbrate</del></td><td>/ˈædəmbreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预示</td><td>adumbrate future trouble<br>预示日后的麻烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The early chapters adumbrate the tragedy that follows.  开头几章预示了随后的悲剧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overshadow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈʃædoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使相形失色；使蒙上阴影</td><td>overshadow her earlier work<br>使她早期作品相形失色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her later novels overshadow the modest work that first made her famous.  她后来的小说使那部最初让她成名的平实作品相形失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ecstasy</b></td><td>/ˈekstəsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>狂喜</td><td>in a state of ecstasy<br>处于狂喜之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd rose in a state of ecstasy when the winning goal went in.  制胜球进网时，全场观众狂喜地站了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elation</td><td>/ɪˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>得意，兴高采烈</td><td>a feeling of elation<br>兴高采烈之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her elation faded once she read the fine print carefully.  她仔细读了小字条款后，兴奋就消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 euphoria</td><td>/juːˈfɔːriə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度愉快，狂喜</td><td>a mood of national euphoria<br>全国上下的狂喜情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The euphoria after the victory faded within a couple of days.  胜利后的极度愉快在几天之内就消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhilaration</td><td>/ɪɡˌzɪləˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋，极度愉快</td><td>a sense of exhilaration<br>一种兴奋感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt pure exhilaration as the glider rose above the valley.  滑翔机升到山谷上方时，她感到无比兴奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapture</td><td>/ˈræptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度欢喜，狂喜</td><td>listen in rapture<br>如痴如醉地聆听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience listened in rapture as the old singer began her final song.  老歌手唱起最后一曲时，观众听得如痴如醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transport</td><td>/trænsˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>运输，运送</td><td>transport the goods<br>运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trucks transport fresh produce to the city every morning.  卡车每天早晨把新鲜农产品运往城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depression</td><td>/dɪˈpreʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>沮丧，抑郁；萧条</td><td>sink into deep depression<br>陷入深度抑郁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing his job he slid into a long depression.  失业之后，他陷入了长久的抑郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ecumenical</b></td><td>/ˌiːkjuːˈmenɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>世界范围的，普遍性的</td><td>an ecumenical council<br>世界性的宗教会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An ecumenical council brought together leaders from every branch of the church.  一次世界性的宗教会议把教会各支派的领袖聚在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 universal</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈvɜːrsəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，全体的</td><td>universal human rights<br>普世人权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Music seems to have a universal appeal across all cultures.  音乐似乎在所有文化中都有普遍的感染力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 global</td><td>/ˈɡloʊbəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>全球的；全面的</td><td>the global economy<br>全球经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pandemic reshaped the global economy in only two years.  疫情仅两年就重塑了全球经济。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 planetary</td><td>/ˈplænəteri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>全球性的；行星的</td><td>planetary motion around the sun<br>行星绕日运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Climate change is now treated as a planetary emergency by most scientists.  多数科学家现在把气候变化视为全球性紧急事态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worldwide</td><td>/ˈwɜːrldwaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>全世界的，遍及全球的</td><td>worldwide recognition<br>世界性的声誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film earned worldwide recognition after winning the festival prize.  这部影片获奖后赢得了世界性的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insular</td><td>/ˈɪnsələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（观念、想法等）孤立狭隘的</td><td>an insular attitude<br>狭隘的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insular attitude toward foreign customs cost him many friendships abroad.  他对外国习俗的狭隘态度使他在国外失去了许多友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>edible</b></td><td>/ˈedəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可食用的</td><td>edible wild mushrooms<br>可食用的野生蘑菇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guide showed us which edible wild mushrooms grow beneath these pines.  导游告诉我们这些松树下长着哪些可食用的野生蘑菇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eatable</td><td>/ˈiːtəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可以吃的，尚能入口的</td><td>barely eatable leftovers<br>勉强能吃的剩饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soup was salty but still eatable after we added water.  这汤很咸，加了水之后还算能吃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 comestible</del></td><td>/kəˈmɛstəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可食用的；食品</td><td>assorted comestible goods<br>各类食品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market sells every comestible item a village kitchen might need.  这个市场出售村里厨房可能需要的各种食品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consumable</td><td>/kənˈsuːməbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可消耗的；消费品</td><td>consumable office supplies<br>办公消耗用品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The budget separates equipment costs from consumable materials like paper and ink.  预算把设备费用与纸墨等消耗品分开列出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 digestible</td><td>/daɪˈdʒestəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易消化的；易理解的</td><td>easily digestible food<br>易消化的食物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report was rewritten in short, digestible sections for busy readers.  报告被改写成简短易懂的小节，方便忙碌的读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 esculent</del></td><td>/ˈeskjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可食用的；食用植物</td><td>esculent roots and berries<br>可食用的根茎与浆果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early settlers learned which esculent plants grew along the riverbank.  早期定居者学会辨认河岸上哪些植物可以食用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inedible</td><td>/ɪnˈedəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不能吃的，不宜食用的</td><td>tough and inedible meat<br>又硬又难以下口的肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The berries look attractive but are completely inedible for humans.  这些浆果看着诱人，但人完全不能吃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>edifice</b></td><td>/ˈedɪfɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大厦；大建筑物；基础，基本构架</td><td>a towering stone edifice<br>高耸的石造大厦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A towering stone edifice now stands where the old market once stood.  高耸的石造大厦如今矗立在旧集市的原址上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 building</td><td>/ˈbɪldɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>建造；建筑物</td><td>the building of a bridge<br>桥梁的建造</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The building of the new bridge took almost four years.  新桥的建造花了将近四年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erection</td><td>/ɪˈrekʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>建立，竖立</td><td>the erection of a monument<br>纪念碑的竖立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The erection of the monument took the workers six months.  纪念碑的竖立花了工人六个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palace</td><td>/ˈpæləs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宫殿，豪华宅邸</td><td>the royal summer palace<br>皇家夏宫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourists queue for hours to enter the palace and see the throne room.  游客排队几小时才能进宫殿参观王座厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 architecture</td><td>/ˈɑːrkɪtektʃər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>建筑；建筑风格；架构</td><td>modern Japanese architecture<br>日本现代建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She studies the architecture of medieval churches in northern Spain.  她研究西班牙北部中世纪教堂的建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 configuration</td><td>/kənˌfɪɡjəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>构造，布局，配置</td><td>the configuration of the network<br>网络的配置结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers changed the configuration of the engine to reduce noise.  工程师改变了发动机的构造以降低噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 framework</td><td>/ˈfreɪmˌwɜːrk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>框架，构架；体系</td><td>a legal framework for reform<br>改革的法律框架</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty provides a framework for cooperation between the two countries.  该条约为两国合作提供了框架。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skeleton</td><td>/ˈskelətən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骨架；框架，梗概</td><td>the tower's steel skeleton<br>塔的钢结构骨架</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the concrete skeleton of the unfinished hotel still stands.  未完工的酒店只剩下混凝土骨架还立在那里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 structure</td><td>/ˈstrʌktʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>结构，构造；建筑物</td><td>the structure of the argument<br>论证的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay has a clear structure with three supporting examples.  这篇文章结构清晰，配有三个支撑例证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cottage</td><td>/ˈkɑːtədʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小屋，村舍</td><td>a small country cottage<br>乡间小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rented a stone cottage near the lake for the summer.  他们租了湖边一间石头小屋过夏天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>efface</b></td><td>/ɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦掉，抹去；使不显眼</td><td>efface all traces<br>抹去所有痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time had effaced the inscription on the old gravestone.  时间已把这块老墓碑上的铭文磨去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eradicate</td><td>/ɪˈrædəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，消灭</td><td>eradicate rural poverty<br>根除农村贫困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vaccination campaigns helped eradicate smallpox across the world.  接种运动帮助在全世界根除了天花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erase</td><td>/ɪˈreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦除，抹去</td><td>erase the pencil marks<br>擦掉铅笔痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to erase every trace of the message from the whiteboard.  他试图把白板上那条信息的痕迹全部擦掉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expunge</td><td>/ɪkˈspʌndʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>删除，消除</td><td>expunge a criminal record<br>消除犯罪记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court agreed to expunge the conviction from her official file.  法庭同意将该定罪从她的正式档案中删除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exterminate</td><td>/ɪkˈstɜːrmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，消灭</td><td>exterminate the entire colony<br>消灭整个群落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team worked for months to exterminate the invasive beetles.  团队花了几个月消灭那些入侵的甲虫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extirpate</td><td>/ˈekstərˌpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，灭绝</td><td>extirpate an invasive species<br>根除入侵物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers worked for years to extirpate the weed from their fields.  农民花了多年才把这种杂草从田里根除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liquidate</td><td>/ˈlɪkwɪˌdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>肃清，消灭；清算</td><td>liquidate political opponents<br>肃清政治对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to liquidate every opponent who remained in the capital.  该政权试图肃清留在首都的每一个反对者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eclipse</td><td>/ɪˈklɪps/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使声望下降，使黯然失色</td><td>eclipse his earlier fame<br>使他早年的名声黯然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His later failures eclipsed the fame he had earned as a young pianist.  他后来的失败使他年轻时作为钢琴家赢得的名声黯然失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obliterate</td><td>/əˈblɪtəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抹去；彻底消灭</td><td>obliterate all traces of it<br>抹去它的一切痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The March blizzard obliterated all traces of the early spring flowers.  三月的暴风雪抹去了早春花朵的一切痕迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wipe</td><td>/ˈwaɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦，抹；擦除</td><td>wipe the slate clean<br>既往不咎，重新开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please wipe the table before you set out the plates.  摆盘子之前请把桌子擦一下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 root out</td><td>/ˈruːt ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>根除，彻底清除</td><td>root out official corruption<br>根除官场腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director promised to root out waste in every department.  新主任承诺根除各部门的浪费现象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blazon</td><td>/ˈbleɪzn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使知名；修饰，装扮</td><td>blazon the news abroad<br>四处宣扬消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper was eager to blazon the news across every front page.  该报急于把这条消息在各家头版上四处宣扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decorate</td><td>/ˈdekəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，布置；授勋</td><td>decorate the room<br>装饰房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children helped decorate the room with paper lanterns.  孩子们帮忙用纸灯笼装饰房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>effervesce</b></del></td><td>/ˌefərˈves/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>兴奋，热情洋溢</td><td>effervesce with excitement<br>兴奋得沸腾起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children effervesced with excitement as the parade turned onto their street.  游行队伍拐进他们那条街时，孩子们兴奋得沸腾起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exult</td><td>/ɪɡˈzʌlt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>感到欢喜</td><td>exult in her victory<br>为她的胜利而欢喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fans exulted in the victory long after the final whistle.  终场哨响很久之后球迷们仍在为胜利欢喜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jubilate</td><td>/ˈdʒubəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欢欣，喜悦</td><td>jubilate over the news<br>为这消息而欢欣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole town began to jubilate when the rescue team returned safely.  救援队平安归来时，全城开始欢庆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 triumph</td><td>/ˈtraɪəmf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>巨大成功，胜利</td><td>a stunning military triumph<br>惊人的军事胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new bridge is a triumph of modern engineering and careful planning.  这座新桥是现代工程与周密规划的巨大成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grieve</td><td>/ɡriːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>（使）感到悲伤</td><td>grieve for a lost friend<br>为逝去的朋友悲伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village grieved for the fishermen lost in the autumn storm.  全村人都为在秋季风暴中失踪的渔民而悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>effete</b></td><td>/ɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，衰落的</td><td>an effete aristocracy<br>衰落的贵族阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effete aristocracy clung to its titles long after the wealth had vanished.  衰落的贵族阶层在财富早已消失后仍死守头衔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consumed</td><td>/kənˈsuːmd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗尽的；被（情绪）吞噬的</td><td>consumed by jealousy<br>被嫉妒吞噬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was consumed by guilt for weeks after the accident.  事故之后他被负罪感折磨了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitated</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，衰弱的</td><td>debilitated by long illness<br>因久病而衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient looked pale and debilitated after months of treatment.  经过数月治疗，病人显得苍白虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decadent</td><td>/ˈdekədənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，腐败的</td><td>a decadent ruling class<br>腐败堕落的统治阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the court as decadent, idle, and blind to reform.  他形容那个宫廷腐败堕落、无所作为、对改革视而不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depleted</td><td>/dɪˈpliːtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗尽的，枯竭的</td><td>severely depleted fish stocks<br>严重枯竭的鱼类资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of overfarming left the soil badly depleted of nutrients.  多年过度耕作使土壤养分严重枯竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drained</td><td>/ˈdreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精疲力竭的；被排空的</td><td>feel completely drained<br>感到精疲力竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the twelve-hour shift she felt utterly drained.  上了十二小时的班，她感到彻底精疲力竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhausted</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːstɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲惫不堪的；耗尽的</td><td>exhausted after the climb<br>登山后疲惫不堪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hikers arrived exhausted but happy at the mountain hut.  徒步者们疲惫却愉快地到达了山中小屋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hale</td><td>/heɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，健壮的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>身体强健，精神饱满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale enough to walk five miles a day.  八十岁的他依然强健，每天能走五英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>effluvium</b></td><td>/ɪˈfluːviəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>难闻的气味</td><td>a foul effluvium<br>难闻的臭气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A foul effluvium rose from the ditch behind the abandoned tannery.  废弃制革厂后面的沟里升起难闻的臭气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 malodor</del></td><td>/mæˈloʊdər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶臭，臭气</td><td>the malodor of stale grease<br>陈腐油脂的恶臭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A faint malodor drifted from the drain behind the restaurant.  餐馆后面的下水道飘来一股淡淡的恶臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stink</td><td>/ˈstɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大声抗议，抱怨；臭味</td><td>raise a stink about it<br>为此大吵大闹抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents raised a stink about the sudden tuition increase.  家长们为学费突然上涨大闹抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stench</td><td>/stentʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>臭气，恶臭</td><td>an unbearable stench<br>难以忍受的恶臭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unbearable stench rose from the drains during the hot afternoon.  炎热的午后，下水道里升起难以忍受的恶臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fragrance</td><td>/ˈfreɪɡrəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>香味，芬芳</td><td>the fragrance of fresh lilacs<br>新鲜紫丁香的芬芳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fragrance of jasmine filled the courtyard every evening.  每天傍晚，茉莉的香味充满庭院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aroma</td><td>/əˈroʊmə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（尤指食物的）香气</td><td>the aroma of fresh coffee<br>新鲜咖啡的香气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aroma of baking bread drew customers into the little shop.  烤面包的香气把顾客引进了这家小店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>effrontery</b></td><td>/ɪˈfrʌntəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厚颜无耻，放肆大胆</td><td>have the effrontery to lie<br>竟然厚颜无耻地撒谎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had the effrontery to lie about the accident right to my face.  他竟厚颜无耻地当着我的面对那场事故说谎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 audacity</td><td>/ɔːˈdæsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大胆无礼，鲁莽</td><td>the audacity of his demands<br>他要求之大胆无礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had the audacity to correct the professor in front of the class.  她竟敢在全班面前纠正教授。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brashness</td><td>/ˈbræʃnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鲁莽放肆，无礼</td><td>youthful brashness<br>年轻人的鲁莽放肆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brashness in meetings offended several senior partners.  他在会上的放肆无礼得罪了几位资深合伙人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brazenness</td><td>/ˈbreɪzənəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厚颜无耻，肆无忌惮</td><td>the brazenness of the theft<br>这起盗窃的肆无忌惮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters were stunned by the brazenness of his denial.  记者们对他否认时的厚颜无耻感到震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insolence</td><td>/ˈɪnsələns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>傲慢无礼，蛮横</td><td>punished for his insolence<br>因傲慢无礼受罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter's insolence ruined an otherwise pleasant evening.  服务员的傲慢无礼毁了本该愉快的一晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nerve</td><td>/nɜːrv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（坚强的）意志，勇气</td><td>lose one's nerve<br>失去勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lost his nerve at the last moment and never asked the question.  他在最后一刻失去了勇气，始终没有问出那个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumption</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冒失无礼；假定</td><td>forgive my presumption<br>请原谅我的冒失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was sheer presumption to speak for the whole committee.  代表整个委员会发言纯属冒失无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temerity</td><td>/təˈmerəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鲁莽，冒失</td><td>have the temerity to ask<br>竟然冒失地开口询问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had the temerity to ask his boss for another raise.  他竟然冒失地向老板再要一次加薪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decorum</td><td>/dɪˈkɔːrəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>礼貌得体，端庄</td><td>behave with proper decorum<br>举止得体有礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests were asked to observe decorum during the memorial service.  来宾被要求在追悼会上保持得体举止。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 propriety</td><td>/prəˈpraɪəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>适当，得体</td><td>a sense of propriety<br>得体的分寸感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sense of propriety kept her from raising the subject at dinner.  她的得体分寸感使她没有在晚餐时提起这个话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 courtesy</td><td>/ˈkɜːrtəsi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>礼貌，谦恭</td><td>common courtesy<br>基本礼貌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had the courtesy to apologize for arriving so late.  她很有礼貌地为迟到道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grace</td><td>/ˈɡreɪs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>优雅得体；风度</td><td>accept defeat with grace<br>得体地接受失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the awkward question with unusual grace.  她极为得体地应对了那个尴尬的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>effulgent</b></td><td>/ɪˈfʌldʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光辉灿烂的</td><td>an effulgent sunrise<br>灿烂的日出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effulgent sunrise turned the snow on the eastern ridges pale gold.  灿烂的日出把东侧山脊上的积雪染成浅金色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beaming</td><td>/ˈbiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>光芒四射的；笑容满面的</td><td>a beaming smile<br>灿烂的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted us with a beaming smile at the airport gate.  她在机场门口用灿烂的笑容迎接我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorious</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːriəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辉煌的，壮丽的</td><td>a glorious summer sunset<br>壮丽的夏日落日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mountains looked glorious in the late afternoon light.  傍晚的光线下群山显得壮丽辉煌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luminous</td><td>/ˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，明亮的</td><td>a luminous dial<br>夜光表盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luminous dial of his watch glowed faintly in the dark.  他手表的夜光表盘在黑暗中微微发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 radiant</td><td>/ˈreɪdiənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光芒四射的；喜气洋洋的</td><td>a radiant smile<br>灿烂的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bride looked radiant as she walked into the garden.  新娘走进花园时神采飞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendid</td><td>/ˈsplendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的；极好的</td><td>a splendid marble hall<br>壮丽的大理石厅堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the roof we had a splendid view of the harbor.  从屋顶望去，港口的景色极为壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 murky</td><td>/ˈmɜːrki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>昏暗的，阴暗的；晦涩不清的</td><td>a murky, windowless basement<br>昏暗无窗的地下室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The murky basement was lit by one failing bulb.  昏暗的地下室里只有一只快坏的灯泡照明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>effusive</b></td><td>/ɪˈfjuːsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情过度外露的，热情洋溢的</td><td>effusive praise for the film<br>对影片过度的赞美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal offered effusive praise for the graduating class's achievements.  校长对毕业班的成就大加赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emotional</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情丰富的；动情的</td><td>an emotional farewell speech<br>动情的告别演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became emotional when describing his father's last letter.  描述父亲最后一封信时他动了感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expansive</td><td>/ɪkˈspænsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的，健谈的</td><td>in an expansive mood<br>谈兴正浓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After dinner he grew expansive and told stories all evening.  晚饭后他变得健谈，整晚讲故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrative</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːnstrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情外露的</td><td>a demonstrative greeting<br>热情外露的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her demonstrative hugs surprised colleagues used to formal handshakes.  她热情的拥抱让习惯正式握手的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gushy</td><td>/ˈɡʌʃi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情流露过度的，肉麻的</td><td>a gushy thank-you note<br>肉麻的感谢便条</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her gushy introduction embarrassed the guest speaker.  她过度热情的介绍让客座讲者很不好意思。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passionate</td><td>/ˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满激情的</td><td>a passionate defense of art<br>对艺术的热情辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is passionate about protecting the city's old neighborhoods.  她对保护城市的老街区充满热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inhibited</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbətɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>拘谨的，压抑的</td><td>too inhibited to speak<br>拘谨得不敢开口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New students often feel inhibited during the first seminar.  新生在第一次讨论课上常感到拘谨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reserved</td><td>/rɪˈzɜːrvd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向的，缄默的</td><td>a quiet, reserved man<br>安静内向的男子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her father was a reserved man who rarely spoke about his feelings.  她的父亲是个内向的人，很少谈论自己的感受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 restrained</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受约束的，克制的</td><td>restrained by rules<br>受规则约束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His restrained reply hid how angry he actually felt inside.  他克制的回答掩盖了他内心真正的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>egalitarian</b></td><td>/iˌɡælɪˈteriən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平等主义的</td><td>an egalitarian society<br>平等主义的社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They founded an egalitarian society in which every member had one vote.  他们建立了一个平等主义的社会，每个成员都只有一票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disinterested</td><td>/dɪsˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，无偏见的</td><td>a disinterested judgment<br>公正的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a disinterested judgment from someone with no stake in the outcome.  我们需要一个与结果无涉的人作出公正的判断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impartial</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːrʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，不偏不倚的</td><td>an impartial observer<br>公正的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute was settled by an impartial third party.  争端由一个公正的第三方解决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unprejudiced</td><td>/ʌnˈprɛdʒədɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unprejudiced view<br>无偏见的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only an unprejudiced reader can judge the argument fairly.  只有无偏见的读者才能公正评判这个论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbiased</td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无偏见的，公正的</td><td>an unbiased opinion<br>无偏见的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need an unbiased assessment of the school's performance.  我们需要对这所学校表现的公正评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 objective</td><td>/əbˈdʒektɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>客观的，公正的；目标</td><td>an objective assessment<br>客观的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters are expected to remain objective about the candidates.  记者应对候选人保持客观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inequitable</td><td>/ɪnˈekwətəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不公平的，不公正的</td><td>an inequitable distribution<br>不公平的分配</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers called the water quotas deeply inequitable.  农民称这些用水配额极不公平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discriminating</td><td>/dɪsˈkrɪməˌneɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有差别对待的；有鉴别力的</td><td>policies discriminating against women<br>歧视女性的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court struck down policies discriminating against women in hiring.  法院废除了在招聘中歧视女性的政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfair</td><td>/ʌnˈfer/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不公平的，不公正的</td><td>an unfair distribution of wealth<br>财富分配不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers complained that the new bonus system was deeply unfair.  工人抱怨新的奖金制度极不公平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>egoistic</b></td><td>/ˌiːɡoʊˈɪstɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>利己的，以自我为中心的</td><td>egoistic motives behind it<br>其背后的利己动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics detected egoistic motives behind his sudden generosity toward the museum.  批评者察觉到他突然对博物馆慷慨背后的利己动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 egocentric</td><td>/ˌiːɡoʊˈsentrɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我中心的，自私的</td><td>an egocentric young star<br>自我中心的年轻明星</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His egocentric behavior slowly drove his teammates away.  他自我中心的行为慢慢把队友都赶走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-centered</td><td>/ˌselfˈsentərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我中心的，自私的</td><td>a self-centered child<br>自我中心的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is so self-centered that he never asks how others feel.  他极其自我中心，从不问别人的感受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 individualistic</td><td>/ˌɪndɪvɪdʒuəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>个人主义的，突出自我的</td><td>a highly individualistic culture<br>高度个人主义的文化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her individualistic approach clashed with the team's shared plan.  她突出自我的做法与团队的共同计划相冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 navel-gazing</td><td>/ˈneɪvəl ˌɡeɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>自我沉溺，只顾自省</td><td>endless political navel-gazing<br>政界无休止的自我纠缠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party spent a year on navel-gazing instead of policy.  该党花了一年时间自我纠缠，而非制定政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 altruistic</td><td>/ˌæltruˈɪstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无私的，利他的</td><td>altruistic motives<br>利他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her altruistic work in the clinic earned wide public respect.  她在诊所无私的工作赢得了公众广泛尊敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 selfless</td><td>/ˈselfləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无私的，不自私的</td><td>selfless devotion to others<br>对他人的无私奉献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors praised her selfless care for the elderly couple.  邻居们称赞她对那对老夫妇的无私照料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elaborate</b></td><td>/ɪˈlæbəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>详细阐述；精心制作的</td><td>elaborate on a point<br>详述某一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you elaborate on your last point a little?  你能就最后一点再详细说明一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circumstantial</td><td>/ˌsɜːrkəmˈstænʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>详细的；根据推断的</td><td>a circumstantial account<br>详尽的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave a long, circumstantial description of that evening.  证人对那个晚上作了长篇详尽的描述。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complex</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpleks/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>复杂的；综合体</td><td>a complex argument<br>复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper presents a complex argument about ancient trade routes.  这篇论文提出了关于古代商路的复杂论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicated</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，难懂的</td><td>a complicated process<br>复杂的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax rules are so complicated that most people hire help.  税务规定如此复杂，多数人都请人帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intricate</td><td>/ˈɪntrəkət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，精细的</td><td>an intricate pattern<br>精细的图案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The carpet shows an intricate pattern of leaves and birds.  地毯上是精细复杂的叶与鸟图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knotty</td><td>/ˈnɑːti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，困难的</td><td>a knotty question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a knotty question that nobody could answer confidently.  教授提出了一个没人能自信回答的棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 minute</td><td>/maɪˈnjuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微不足道的；细致的</td><td>a minute quantity<br>极小的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even a minute trace of the chemical can spoil the batch.  即使极微量的这种化学品也会毁掉整批产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 particular</td><td>/pərˈtɪkjələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>详细的；特定的；讲究的</td><td>a particular account of events<br>对事件的详细说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a particular account of every repair the house needed.  他详细说明了房子所需的每一项修缮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 particularized</td><td>/pərˈtɪkjələraɪzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>详述的，逐项列明的</td><td>a particularized cost estimate<br>逐项列明的成本估算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The auditor demanded a particularized record of every payment.  审计员要求提供每笔付款的逐项记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticated</td><td>/səˈfɪstɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂精密的；老练的</td><td>a sophisticated system<br>复杂精密的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab uses highly sophisticated equipment to detect the particles.  实验室用极为精密的设备探测这些粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 develop</td><td>/dɪˈveləp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>发展，培养</td><td>develop a skill<br>培养一项技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She developed a habit of reading for an hour each night.  她养成了每晚阅读一小时的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amplify</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大，增强；详述</td><td>amplify a signal<br>放大信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you amplify your last remark a little?  你能把最后那句话再详述一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explain</td><td>/ɪkˈspleɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explain the reason<br>解释原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you explain why the experiment failed the second time?  你能解释一下第二次实验为何失败吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sketchy</td><td>/ˈsketʃi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗略的，不详尽的</td><td>only sketchy details available<br>只有粗略的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early reports about the fire were sketchy and often wrong.  关于火灾的早期报道很粗略，还常有错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compendious</td><td>/kəmˈpɛndiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明扼要的</td><td>a compendious reference guide<br>简明扼要的参考手册</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote a compendious history of the city in ninety pages.  他用九十页写成了一部简明的城市史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 summary</td><td>/ˈsʌməri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>概括的，简略的；摘要</td><td>a summary of the report<br>报告的摘要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please attach a one-page summary to the front of the proposal.  请在提案前面附上一页摘要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downplay</td><td>/ˌdaʊnˈpleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写，不予重视</td><td>downplay the significance<br>淡化其重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He downplayed his own role in the company's success.  他淡化了自己在公司成功中的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 neglect</td><td>/nəˈɡlekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>忽视，疏忽；忽略，怠慢</td><td>neglect his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He neglected his duties for weeks and was finally dismissed.  他数周疏忽职责，最终被解雇了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elastic</b></td><td>/ɪˈlæstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有弹性的；灵活的</td><td>an elastic waistband<br>有弹性的腰带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These trousers have an elastic waistband, so they fit almost anyone.  这条裤子有松紧腰带，几乎任何人都能穿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bouncy</td><td>/ˈbaʊnsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有弹性的；活泼的</td><td>a bouncy rubber ball<br>弹性很好的橡皮球</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children loved the bouncy mattress in the guest room.  孩子们很喜欢客房里那张弹性十足的床垫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malleable</td><td>/ˈmæliəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可塑的</td><td>malleable metals like gold<br>像黄金一样可塑的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gold and other malleable metals can be hammered into thin sheets.  黄金和其他易延展的金属可以被锤成薄片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resilient</td><td>/rɪˈzɪljənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有弹性的；能迅速恢复的</td><td>a resilient local economy<br>恢复力强的地方经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children are often more resilient than their parents expect.  孩子往往比父母想象的更有恢复力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stretchable</td><td>/ˈstrɛtʃəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可伸展的，有弹性的</td><td>a stretchable fabric cover<br>可伸展的布套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chair comes with a stretchable cover that fits any shape.  这椅子配有可伸展的椅套，什么形状都能套上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supple</td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adaptable</td><td>/əˈdæptəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>适应力强的；可改编的</td><td>a highly adaptable species<br>适应力极强的物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crows are adaptable birds that thrive even in crowded cities.  乌鸦适应力很强，连拥挤的城市里也能繁盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjustable</td><td>/əˈdʒʌstəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可调节的</td><td>an adjustable office chair<br>可调节的办公椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The desk has adjustable legs so it suits both children and adults.  这张桌子的腿可调节，大人小孩都合用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buoyant</td><td>/ˈbuːjənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心情好的</td><td>in a buoyant mood<br>心情愉快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a buoyant mood after hearing the good news.  听到好消息后，他心情愉快地到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 volatile</td><td>/ˈvɑːlətl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多变的</td><td>a volatile stock market<br>多变的股市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors hesitated because the stock market stayed volatile all spring.  由于股市整个春天都动荡多变，投资者犹豫不决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 established</td><td>/ɪˈstæblɪʃt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>已确立的，根深蒂固的</td><td>an established tradition<br>已确立的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm has an established reputation for careful engineering.  这家公司在精细工程方面已有稳固声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fixed</td><td>/ˈfɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定的，不变的</td><td>a fixed monthly rate<br>固定的月费率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tenants pay a fixed rent for the first three years.  租户前三年支付固定租金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immutable</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可变的</td><td>an immutable law of nature<br>不可变的自然法则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gravity is an immutable law that no clever argument can suspend.  引力是不可变的法则，任何巧辩都无法使它失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>elate</b></del></td><td>/iˈleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使欢欣，使得意</td><td>elate the whole team<br>使全队欢欣自豪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unexpected award elated the whole team for the rest of the season.  这个意外的奖项让全队在赛季余下的时间里都欢欣自豪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 exhilarate</del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪləreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使高兴；使兴奋</td><td>exhilarate the audience<br>使观众兴奋不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fast ride down the mountain exhilarated everyone in the car.  沿山路飞驰而下让车里的每个人都兴奋不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inspire</td><td>/ɪnˈspaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓舞，激励；引发</td><td>inspire confidence in others<br>让他人产生信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech inspired the students to work much harder.  她的演讲激励学生更加努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depress</td><td>/dɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低，使降低；使沮丧</td><td>depress house prices<br>压低房价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A weak harvest can depress prices for the whole season.  歉收会压低整个季度的价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dishearten</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使失去信心</td><td>disheartened by the news<br>因这消息而沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The string of early losses disheartened even the most loyal fans.  开局连输让最忠实的球迷也失去了信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sadden</td><td>/ˈsædən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使悲伤</td><td>sadden her deeply<br>使她深感悲伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of his death saddened the entire small village.  他去世的消息让整个小村庄都感到悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>eleemosynary</b></del></td><td>/ˌeləˈmɑːsəneri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慈善的，施舍的</td><td>an eleemosynary institution<br>慈善机构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hospital began as an eleemosynary institution for the poor.  这家医院最初是为穷人设立的慈善机构。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 benevolent</td><td>/bəˈnevələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，行善的</td><td>a benevolent employer<br>仁慈的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A benevolent stranger paid for the family's train tickets.  一位仁慈的陌生人为这家人买了火车票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humane</td><td>/hjuˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>人道的，仁慈的</td><td>humane treatment of prisoners<br>人道地对待囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Activists demanded more humane conditions inside the detention center.  活动人士要求改善拘留中心内的人道条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humanitarian</td><td>/ˌhjuːmænəˈteriən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>人道主义的，博爱的</td><td>urgent humanitarian aid<br>紧急人道主义援助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several agencies sent humanitarian supplies to the flooded region.  几家机构向洪灾地区运送了人道主义物资。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 philanthropic</td><td>/ˌfɪlənˈθrɑːpɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>博爱的，为他人着想的</td><td>philanthropic donations<br>慈善捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Philanthropic donations paid for the new hospital wing for sick children.  慈善捐赠支付了收治病童的医院新翼楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parsimonious</td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elegy</b></td><td>/ˈelədʒi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>哀歌（诗），挽歌（诗）</td><td>write an elegy for him<br>为他写一首挽歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote a short elegy for the friend who died in the flood.  他为在洪水中丧生的朋友写了一首短短的挽歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dirge</td><td>/ˈdɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>哀歌，葬礼曲</td><td>a slow funeral dirge<br>缓慢的葬礼哀歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band played a mournful dirge as the coffin passed.  棺木经过时，乐队奏起哀伤的挽歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lamentation</td><td>/ˌlæmɛnˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>哀叹，悲恸</td><td>loud cries of lamentation<br>响亮的哀恸之声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village filled with lamentation when news of the wreck arrived.  沉船的消息传来时，村里满是哀恸之声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requiem</td><td>/ˈrekwiəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安魂曲，追思曲</td><td>sing a solemn requiem<br>唱一首庄严的安魂曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir performed a requiem in memory of the fallen miners.  合唱团演唱安魂曲，纪念遇难的矿工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ode</td><td>/ˈoʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>颂歌，颂诗</td><td>an ode to autumn<br>一首秋之颂歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet wrote an ode celebrating the river of his childhood.  诗人写了一首颂诗，赞美童年的那条河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elephantine</b></td><td>/ˌelɪˈfæntiːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的；笨拙的</td><td>elephantine movements<br>笨重的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His elephantine movements on stage made the delicate dance look almost comic.  他在台上笨重的动作让这支细腻的舞蹈显得近乎滑稽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 huge</td><td>/ˈhjuːdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，庞大的</td><td>a huge success<br>巨大的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A huge crowd waited outside the stadium in the rain.  一大群人在雨中等在体育场外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 colossal</td><td>/kəˈlɑːsl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a colossal statue<br>巨大的雕像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A colossal statue of the founder stands in the square.  广场上矗立着创始人的巨大雕像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enormous</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːrməs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，庞大的</td><td>an enormous amount of data<br>庞大的数据量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project required an enormous amount of money and patience.  这个项目需要巨额资金和极大耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gargantuan</td><td>/ɡɑːrˈɡæntʃuən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a gargantuan appetite<br>巨大的食量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young athlete has a gargantuan appetite after every practice.  这名年轻运动员每次训练后都有巨大的食量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gigantic</td><td>/dʒaɪˈɡæntɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a gigantic stone statue<br>巨大的石像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gigantic stone statue guards the entrance to the temple.  一座巨大的石像守卫着神庙的入口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 massive</td><td>/ˈmæsɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，厚重的</td><td>a massive iron gate<br>厚重的铁门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A massive wave struck the harbor wall just after midnight.  午夜刚过，一个巨浪撞上了防波堤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prodigious</td><td>/prəˈdɪdʒəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的；惊人的，了不起的</td><td>a prodigious memory<br>惊人的记忆力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pianist has a prodigious memory for long and complicated scores.  这位钢琴家对冗长复杂的乐谱有惊人的记忆力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tremendous</td><td>/trəˈmendəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极大的</td><td>a tremendous impact<br>极大的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy had a tremendous effect on small farmers everywhere.  新政策对各地小农户产生了极大的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
