<tr><td>↳近 awkward</td><td>/ˈɔːkwərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的；尴尬的</td><td>an awkward silence<br>尴尬的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awkward silence followed his remark about her divorce.  他谈到她离婚之后，出现了一阵尴尬的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 graceless</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不优雅的</td><td>a graceless heavy stride<br>笨重不雅的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His graceless attempt at an apology made things worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试让事情更糟了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maladroit</td><td>/ˌmæləˈdrɔɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的</td><td>a maladroit attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His maladroit attempt at an apology only made the situation worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试只是让情况变得更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 microscopic</td><td>/ˌmaɪkrəˈskɑːpɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微小的；显微镜下的</td><td>microscopic dust particles<br>微小的尘埃颗粒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists found microscopic cracks in the metal beam.  科学家在金属梁中发现了极微小的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 minute</td><td>/maɪˈnjuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微不足道的；细致的</td><td>a minute quantity<br>极小的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even a minute trace of the chemical can spoil the batch.  即使极微量的这种化学品也会毁掉整批产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tiny</td><td>/ˈtaɪni/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微小的</td><td>a tiny fraction<br>极小的一部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a tiny number of applicants pass the first round.  只有极少数申请者能通过第一轮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infinitesimal</td><td>/ˌɪnfɪnɪˈtesɪməl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极微小的，无穷小的</td><td>an infinitesimal chance<br>微乎其微的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk of infection is infinitesimal if the tools are sterile.  只要器械无菌，感染风险微乎其微。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 graceful</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>优雅的，优美的</td><td>a graceful dancer<br>姿态优雅的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a graceful turn and landed without a sound.  她优雅地一个转身，落地时毫无声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elevate</b></td><td>/ˈelɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，提高；使兴奋</td><td>elevate the mind<br>提升心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good literature can elevate the mind and refine one's taste.  优秀的文学能提升心智、陶冶品味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advance</td><td>/ədˈvæns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>前进；促进；预付款</td><td>advance a theory<br>提出一种理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The army advanced slowly through the thick forest at dawn.  黎明时军队缓慢穿过密林前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ennoble</td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upgrade</td><td>/ˌʌpˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提升，升级</td><td>upgrade the equipment<br>升级设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline upgraded her to business class without any charge.  航空公司免费把她升级到商务舱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 elate</del></td><td>/iˈleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使欢欣，使得意</td><td>elate the whole team<br>使全队欢欣自豪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unexpected award elated the whole team for the rest of the season.  这个意外的奖项让全队在赛季余下的时间里都欢欣自豪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enrapture</del></td><td>/ɪnˈræptʃər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使狂喜，使着迷</td><td>enrapture the whole audience<br>使全场观众入迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist enraptured everyone in the small concert hall.  这位年轻钢琴家让小音乐厅里的每个人都入了迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 exhilarate</del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪləreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使高兴；使兴奋</td><td>exhilarate the audience<br>使观众兴奋不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fast ride down the mountain exhilarated everyone in the car.  沿山路飞驰而下让车里的每个人都兴奋不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intoxicate</td><td>/ɪnˈtɑːksɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使陶醉；使醉</td><td>intoxicate her with praise<br>用赞美使她陶醉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sudden success seemed to intoxicate the young writer completely.  突来的成功似乎让这位年轻作家彻底陶醉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transport</td><td>/trænsˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>运输，运送</td><td>transport the goods<br>运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trucks transport fresh produce to the city every morning.  卡车每天早晨把新鲜农产品运往城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debase</td><td>/dɪˈbeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，贬损</td><td>debase the currency<br>使货币贬值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians who lie to win votes debase the office they hold.  为拉票而撒谎的政客贬损了他们所担任的职务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demote</td><td>/ˌdiːˈmoʊt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>降职，降级</td><td>demote him to clerk<br>把他降为职员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board decided to demote him to clerk after the scandal.  丑闻之后，董事会决定把他降为书记员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depress</td><td>/dɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低，使降低；使沮丧</td><td>depress house prices<br>压低房价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A weak harvest can depress prices for the whole season.  歉收会压低整个季度的价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elicit</b></td><td>/iˈlɪsɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，唤起</td><td>elicit a strong reaction<br>引起强烈反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king's speech elicited sustained cheers and applause from the crowd.  国王的演讲激起了人群持久的欢呼和掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arouse</td><td>/əˈraʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，激起；唤醒</td><td>arouse public suspicion<br>引起公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missing files aroused suspicion among the auditors.  丢失的文件引起了审计人员的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evoke</td><td>/ɪˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，引发</td><td>evoke childhood memories<br>唤起童年回忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of wet chalk evokes my primary school classroom.  湿粉笔的味道让我想起小学的教室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inspire</td><td>/ɪnˈspaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓舞，激励；引发</td><td>inspire confidence in others<br>让他人产生信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech inspired the students to work much harder.  她的演讲激励学生更加努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 appease</td><td>/əˈpiːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；满足</td><td>appease the angry crowd<br>安抚愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered refunds to appease its angry customers.  公司提供退款以安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 placate</td><td>/ˈpleɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过让步以）平息抚慰</td><td>placate the critics<br>平息批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered full refunds to placate its angriest customers.  公司提供全额退款以平息最愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elliptical</b></td><td>/ɪˈlɪptɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>含糊不清的；椭圆的</td><td>an elliptical reply<br>含糊其辞的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her elliptical reply told the reporters almost nothing about the final decision.  她含糊其辞的回答几乎没向记者透露任何关于最终决定的信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambiguous</td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arcane</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的，难以理解的</td><td>arcane rules<br>晦涩难懂的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is full of arcane rules nobody understands.  税法充满无人理解的晦涩规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cryptic</td><td>/ˈkrɪptɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的；难以理解的</td><td>a cryptic remark<br>费解的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His cryptic note gave us no hint about where he had gone.  他那张费解的便条没有透露他去了哪里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enigmatic</td><td>/ˌenɪɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>an enigmatic smile<br>神秘的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enigmatic answer left the reporters guessing for several days.  她神秘的回答让记者们猜了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inscrutable</td><td>/ɪnˈskruːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高深莫测的，难以理解的</td><td>an inscrutable expression<br>高深莫测的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inscrutable smile gave nothing away about his plans.  他那高深莫测的笑容丝毫没有透露他的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murky</td><td>/ˈmɜːrki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>昏暗的，阴暗的；晦涩不清的</td><td>a murky, windowless basement<br>昏暗无窗的地下室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The murky basement was lit by one failing bulb.  昏暗的地下室里只有一只快坏的灯泡照明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nebulous</td><td>/ˈnebjələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的</td><td>a nebulous plan<br>模糊不清的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager presented a nebulous plan with no dates, budgets, or clear goals.  经理提出了一个没有日期、预算或明确目标的模糊计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occult</td><td>/əˈkʌlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>occult knowledge<br>玄奥的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manuscript is filled with occult knowledge that scholars still cannot decipher.  这份手稿满是学者至今无法解读的玄奥知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opaque</td><td>/oʊˈpeɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不透明的；晦涩难懂的</td><td>opaque legal language<br>晦涩的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regulations remain opaque even to experienced tax lawyers.  这些法规连资深税务律师都觉得晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vague</td><td>/veɪɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达不清的</td><td>a vague answer<br>含糊的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His vague answer told the reporters almost nothing about the plan.  他含糊的回答几乎没有向记者透露任何计划内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oval</td><td>/ˈoʊvəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>椭圆形的；椭圆形</td><td>an oval dining table<br>椭圆形餐桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wore a small oval locket on a silver chain.  她戴着一条银链，挂着小小的椭圆吊盒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ovate</td><td>/ˈoʊveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卵形的，蛋形的</td><td>broad ovate leaves<br>宽卵形的叶片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This shrub can be identified by its ovate glossy leaves.  这种灌木可通过其光亮的卵形叶片辨认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explicit</td><td>/ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，明确的</td><td>explicit instructions<br>明确的指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave explicit instructions about how to store the samples.  她就样品的保存方式给出了明确指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unambiguous</td><td>/ˌʌnæmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，毫不含糊的</td><td>unambiguous written instructions<br>明确的书面指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract gives unambiguous rules about late payment.  合同就迟付款给出了明确规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unequivocal</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不含糊的，明确的</td><td>an unequivocal denial<br>毫不含糊的否认</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board issued an unequivocal statement supporting the director.  董事会发表了明确支持主任的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 circular</td><td>/ˈsɜːrkjələr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回的，环形的</td><td>a circular argument<br>循环论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path is circular, so it brings you back here.  这条小路是环形的，会把你带回这里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 round</td><td>/ˈraʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>圆的，圆形的</td><td>a small round window<br>小圆窗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They sat at a round table so everyone could see each other.  他们围着圆桌坐，这样人人都能看见对方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>elucidate</b></td><td>/iˈluːsɪdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>elucidate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The footnotes elucidate the problem far better than the chapter itself does.  脚注比正文本身更好地阐明了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 construe</td><td>/kənˈstruː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，理解为</td><td>construe the remark as praise<br>把那句话理解为赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not construe my silence as agreement with the plan.  别把我的沉默理解为赞同这个计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explain</td><td>/ɪkˈspleɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explain the reason<br>解释原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you explain why the experiment failed the second time?  你能解释一下第二次实验为何失败吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explicate</td><td>/ˈeksplɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explicate a difficult text<br>解说一段难懂的文本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher patiently explicated the most difficult passage in the poem.  老师耐心地解说了这首诗中最难的一段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expound</td><td>/ɪkˈspaʊnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>详细阐述，解释</td><td>expound on his theory<br>详细阐述他的理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor expounded her views on urban design for an hour.  教授用一个小时详细阐述了她的城市设计观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illustrate</td><td>/ˈɪləsˌtreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>说明，举例说明</td><td>illustrate the point<br>说明这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These figures illustrate how quickly the population has grown.  这些数字说明人口增长得多么迅速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confuse</td><td>/kənˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；混淆</td><td>confuse the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vague instructions confused nearly everyone in the training class.  含糊的说明让培训班几乎所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obfuscate</td><td>/ˈɑːbfʌskeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使模糊</td><td>obfuscate the facts<br>把事实弄模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed determined to obfuscate the facts rather than explain them.  证人似乎一心要把事实弄模糊，而不是解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>emaciate</b></del></td><td>/ɪˈmeɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使消瘦，使憔悴</td><td>emaciate the patient's body<br>使病人身体消瘦衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of fever had emaciated the patient's body almost beyond recognition.  数月高烧使病人的身体消瘦衰弱得几乎认不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 droop</td><td>/ˈdruːp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低垂，萎靡</td><td>the flowers begin to droop<br>花开始低垂萎蔫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plants drooped badly after three days without water.  三天没浇水，植物都严重萎蔫低垂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flag</td><td>/flæɡ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>his energy began to flag<br>他的精力开始衰减</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His energy began to flag after the twentieth mile.  跑到第二十英里后他的精力开始衰减。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sag</td><td>/ˈsæɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>下垂，下陷；衰退</td><td>the old roof sags badly<br>旧屋顶严重下陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mattress sagged in the middle after years of use.  用了多年后，床垫中间下陷了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languish</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>languish in a damp cell<br>在潮湿牢房中日渐衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners languished in damp cells for months without proper food.  囚犯们在潮湿的牢房里日渐衰弱，数月没有适当的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wither</td><td>/ˈwɪðər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>枯萎，凋谢</td><td>wither in the heat<br>在高温中枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomato plants withered after a week without rain or shade.  一周无雨又没有遮荫，番茄苗都枯萎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>emancipate</b></td><td>/ɪˈmænsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，解除束缚</td><td>emancipate the slaves<br>解放奴隶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law emancipated the slaves working on the northern plantations.  新法律解放了在北方种植园劳作的奴隶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enfranchise</td><td>/ɪnˈfræntʃaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给予…权利（例如选举权）；解放</td><td>enfranchise women<br>赋予妇女选举权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The amendment enfranchised millions of citizens for the first time.  该修正案首次赋予数百万公民选举权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 free</td><td>/ˈfriː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，解放；自由的</td><td>free the hostages<br>释放人质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Negotiators finally persuaded the gunmen to free the hostages.  谈判者最终说服枪手释放了人质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liberate</td><td>/ˈlɪbəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，释放</td><td>liberate the city<br>解放这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Allied troops liberated the city in the spring of that year.  盟军在那年春天解放了这座城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loose</td><td>/ˈluːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，放开；松的</td><td>loose the dogs<br>放开猎狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper loosed the hawk and watched it climb into the sky.  饲养员放开那只鹰，看它飞向天空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 manumit</del></td><td>/ˌmænjuˈmɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放（奴隶）</td><td>manumit his slaves<br>解放他的奴隶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dying planter chose to manumit every slave who had worked his fields.  这位垂死的种植园主决定解放所有为他耕作过的奴隶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 release</td><td>/riˈliːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>释放，解除束缚；发布</td><td>release the prisoners<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ordered officials to release him from custody at once.  法庭命令官员立即将他从拘留中释放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bind</td><td>/ˈbaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>捆绑，束缚；约束</td><td>bind his hands<br>捆住他的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty binds both countries to reduce their emissions.  该条约约束两国削减排放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shackle</td><td>/ˈʃækl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚；限制，阻碍</td><td>shackle free trade<br>束缚自由贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tariffs would shackle trade between the two neighboring countries.  新关税会束缚这两个邻国之间的贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embargo</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbɑːrɡoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>贸易禁止令</td><td>lift the trade embargo<br>解除贸易禁令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two governments agreed to lift the trade embargo after long talks.  经过长时间会谈，两国政府同意解除贸易禁令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ban</td><td>/ˈbæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>禁止；禁令</td><td>ban smoking indoors<br>禁止室内吸烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city voted to ban plastic bags in all supermarkets.  该市投票决定禁止所有超市使用塑料袋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interdiction</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈdɪkʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，阻断，封锁</td><td>drug interdiction efforts<br>禁毒阻断行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Naval interdiction stopped most of the smuggling along the coast.  海军的阻断行动制止了沿岸大部分走私。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proscription</td><td>/proʊˈskrɪpʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，查禁；剥夺权利</td><td>the proscription of books<br>对书籍的查禁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wartime proscription of certain newspapers lasted for several years.  战时对某些报纸的查禁持续了数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veto</td><td>/ˈviːtoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>否决，禁止</td><td>veto the proposed bill<br>否决拟议的法案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The governor promised to veto the bill if it reached his desk.  州长承诺，若这项法案送到他手上就予以否决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approval</td><td>/əˈpruːvəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>批准，认可；赞成</td><td>seek her approval<br>征求她的同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan needs approval from the board before work begins.  该计划开工前需要董事会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 license</td><td>/ˈlaɪsns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许可，认可；自由</td><td>a license to practice<br>执业许可证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally obtained a license to practice medicine in three different states.  她终于取得了在三个不同州行医的执业许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permission</td><td>/pərˈmɪʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>许可，允许</td><td>ask for permission<br>请求许可</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You need permission from the owner before entering the field.  进入这片地之前需要得到主人许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prescription</td><td>/prɪˈskrɪpʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>规定，指令；处方</td><td>the prescription of strict rules<br>严格规则的制定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charter contains a clear prescription of every member's duties.  该章程对每位成员的职责有明确规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embark</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbɑːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>开始从事</td><td>embark on a new career<br>开始新的职业生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At forty she embarked on a new career as a literary translator.  四十岁时她开始从事文学翻译这一新职业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 begin</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手</td><td>begin work on the project<br>开始着手这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We will begin the renovation as soon as the permit arrives.  许可一到我们就开始装修。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commence</td><td>/kəˈmens/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手（正式）</td><td>commence legal proceedings<br>开始法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Construction will commence next month if funding is approved.  若资金获批，工程将于下月开始。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 initiate</td><td>/ɪˈnɪʃieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>创始，发起</td><td>initiate a reform program<br>发起一项改革计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new governor plans to initiate a sweeping reform of rural education.  新州长计划发起一项农村教育的全面改革。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 start</td><td>/ˈstɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>from the start<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the start it was clear the schedule was too tight.  从一开始就明显看出日程太紧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclude</td><td>/kənˈkluːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结束；得出结论</td><td>conclude the ceremony<br>结束仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge concluded that the evidence was too weak.  法官得出结论认为证据太薄弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 finish</td><td>/ˈfɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>完成，结束</td><td>finish the report tonight<br>今晚完成报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hopes to finish her thesis before the winter holiday.  她希望在寒假前完成论文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terminate</td><td>/ˈtɜːrməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>terminate the agreement<br>终止协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club terminated his membership without any explanation.  俱乐部未加解释就终止了他的会员资格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embarrass</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbærəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬</td><td>embarrass him in public<br>在公众面前使他尴尬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to embarrass her in public and only embarrassed himself.  他想在公众面前让她尴尬，结果只是让自己出丑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abash</del></td><td>/əˈbæʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬，使羞愧</td><td>be abashed by praise<br>因受赞扬而羞怯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked abashed when everyone applauded his clumsy speech.  当大家为他笨拙的演讲鼓掌时，他显得很窘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discomfit</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkʌmfɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬；阻碍</td><td>discomfit an opponent<br>使对手窘迫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blunt question visibly discomfited the confident speaker.  这个直率的问题明显让自信的演讲者局促不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconcert</td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈsɜːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使慌乱</td><td>disconcert the audience<br>使观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His long silences disconcerted everyone at the table.  他长时间的沉默让餐桌上所有人都不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faze</td><td>/feɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使慌乱，使窘迫</td><td>nothing seems to faze him<br>似乎没什么能扰乱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The huge crowd did not faze the young pianist at all.  庞大的观众丝毫没让这位年轻钢琴家慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluster</td><td>/ˈflʌstər/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使慌乱，使紧张</td><td>get flustered easily<br>容易慌乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unexpected questions always fluster him during interviews.  面试中意外的问题总让他慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mortify</td><td>/ˈmɔːrtɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使羞愧，使难堪</td><td>be mortified with embarrassment<br>羞愧得无地自容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was mortified to find her mistake printed in the report.  发现自己的错误印在报告里，她羞愧难当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonplus</del></td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈplʌs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使迷惑，使困窘</td><td>be completely nonplussed<br>完全不知所措</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her strange reply left the whole panel nonplussed.  她奇怪的回答让整个评审团都困惑不解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embed</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbed/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嵌入</td><td>embed a tiny chip<br>嵌入一枚微小芯片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers embed a tiny chip in each card to store the account data.  工程师在每张卡里嵌入一枚微小芯片来存储账户数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entrench</td><td>/enˈtrentʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>牢固确立，使根深蒂固</td><td>entrench their own privileges<br>牢固确立自身特权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reforms only entrenched the power of the largest banks.  这些改革只是让大银行的权力更加牢固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingrain</td><td>/ɪnˈɡreɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使根深蒂固，深植</td><td>ingrain a sense of duty<br>把责任感深植于心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of training ingrained in her the habit of checking every figure twice.  多年训练使她养成了把每个数字复查两遍的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 root</td><td>/ˈruːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使扎根；根，根源</td><td>deeply rooted in tradition<br>深深扎根于传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The custom is rooted in the farming life of the valley.  这一习俗根植于山谷的农耕生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extract</td><td>/ɪkˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拔出，抽出；提取</td><td>extract a bad tooth<br>拔掉一颗坏牙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dentist had to extract a badly decayed back tooth.  牙医不得不拔掉一颗严重腐坏的后牙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dislodge</td><td>/dɪsˈlɑːdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>移出，逐出</td><td>dislodge a loose stone<br>撬下一块松动的石头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rescuers used a bar to dislodge the beam trapping his leg.  救援人员用铁棍撬开压住他腿的横梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uproot</td><td>/ʌpˈruːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除；使离开家园</td><td>uproot an old olive tree<br>把老橄榄树连根拔起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm uprooted dozens of trees along the coast road.  风暴把沿海公路旁数十棵树连根拔起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embezzle</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbezl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>盗用，挪用（公款）</td><td>embezzle company funds<br>挪用公司资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The accountant embezzled company funds for nearly six years without detection.  这名会计挪用公司资金近六年而未被发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appropriate</td><td>/əˈproʊprieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>私自挪用；适当的</td><td>appropriate public funds<br>挪用公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials were accused of appropriating public money for private use.  官员们被指控将公款挪为私用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 peculate</del></td><td>/ˈpɛkjəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挪用公款，盗用</td><td>peculate public money<br>盗用公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treasurer was jailed for peculating funds over many years.  财务主管因多年盗用公款而入狱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confiscate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnfəˌskeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>没收，查封</td><td>confiscate smuggled goods<br>没收走私货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Customs officers confiscated the ivory carvings at the airport.  海关人员在机场没收了那些象牙雕件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embolden</b></td><td>/ɪmˈboʊldən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，使大胆</td><td>embolden the rebels<br>壮了叛乱者的胆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early success emboldened her to ask for a promotion.  初期的成功让她有胆量要求升职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animate</td><td>/ˈænəmeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使有生气，激励；有活力的</td><td>animate the discussion<br>让讨论活跃起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few well-chosen questions can animate an otherwise dull discussion.  几个精心挑选的问题就能让本来沉闷的讨论活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hearten</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给予鼓励，鼓舞</td><td>hearten the troops<br>鼓舞部队士气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd's cheering heartened the runners on the last steep hill.  观众的欢呼在最后一个陡坡上鼓舞了跑者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 daunt</td><td>/dɔːnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使胆怯，吓倒</td><td>daunt the bravest soldiers<br>吓倒最勇敢的士兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The steep climb ahead did not daunt the experienced mountain guide.  前方陡峭的攀登丝毫没有吓倒这位经验丰富的山地导游。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discourage</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁；劝阻</td><td>discourage him from trying<br>劝他不要尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early failure should not discourage a young researcher.  早期的失败不该让年轻研究者气馁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dishearten</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使失去信心</td><td>disheartened by the news<br>因这消息而沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The string of early losses disheartened even the most loyal fans.  开局连输让最忠实的球迷也失去了信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dispirit</del></td><td>/dɪˈspɪrɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁</td><td>dispirit the whole team<br>使全队士气低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The endless rain dispirited even the most cheerful campers.  连绵的阴雨让最开朗的营员也沮丧起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>emboss</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbɔːs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压印浮凸图案，使凸起</td><td>emboss the leather cover<br>在皮封面上压出凸纹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The binder chose to emboss the leather cover with a small gold crest.  装订工决定在皮封面上压出一个小小的金色纹章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adorn</td><td>/əˈdɔːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，点缀</td><td>adorn the walls<br>装饰墙面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps and photographs adorn the walls of the study.  旧地图和照片装饰着书房的墙壁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beautify</td><td>/ˈbjuːtɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>美化，装饰</td><td>beautify the city<br>美化城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers planted trees to beautify the neglected riverside park.  志愿者种树来美化那座荒废的河滨公园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bedeck</del></td><td>/bɪˈdek/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，点缀</td><td>bedeck the hall with flowers<br>用花装饰大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers bedeck the hall with flowers before every summer festival.  每年夏季节庆前，村民都用花装饰大厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blazon</td><td>/ˈbleɪzn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使知名；修饰，装扮</td><td>blazon the news abroad<br>四处宣扬消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper was eager to blazon the news across every front page.  该报急于把这条消息在各家头版上四处宣扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ornament</td><td>/ˈɔːrnəˌment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装饰；装饰品</td><td>ornament the hall with flowers<br>用花装饰大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Carved birds ornament the wooden beams of the old temple.  雕刻的飞鸟装饰着古庙的木梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garnish</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装饰，点缀（食物）</td><td>garnish with fresh herbs<br>用新鲜香草点缀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef garnished each plate with parsley and thin lemon slices.  厨师用欧芹和薄柠檬片点缀每个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flatten</td><td>/ˈflætən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平坦，压平</td><td>flatten the cardboard boxes<br>把纸箱压平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers flattened the ground before laying the new track.  工人在铺新轨道前先把地面整平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 efface</td><td>/ɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦掉，抹去；使不显眼</td><td>efface all traces<br>抹去所有痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time had effaced the inscription on the old gravestone.  时间已把这块老墓碑上的铭文磨去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embrace</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>乐于接受；拥抱</td><td>embrace change<br>乐于接受变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers quickly embraced the new technology.  年轻员工迅速接受了这项新技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adopt</td><td>/əˈdɑːpt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>采纳，采用；收养</td><td>adopt a new policy<br>采纳新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to adopt stricter rules on noise at night.  市议会投票采纳了更严格的夜间噪音规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 espouse</td><td>/ɪˈspaʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，拥护</td><td>espouse a cause<br>拥护一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She openly espoused the cause of prison reform.  她公开拥护监狱改革事业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 welcome</td><td>/ˈwelkəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>欢迎，乐于接受</td><td>welcome the chance to help<br>乐于接受帮忙的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Residents welcomed the decision to reopen the public library.  居民们欢迎重新开放公共图书馆的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abjure</td><td>/əbˈdʒʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃；避免</td><td>abjure violence<br>发誓弃绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group publicly abjured violence as a political tool.  该团体公开宣布放弃以暴力作为政治手段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abrogate</td><td>/ˈæbrəɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>正式废除（法律、条约）</td><td>abrogate a treaty<br>废除条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament voted to abrogate the outdated law.  议会投票废除这项过时的法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spurn</td><td>/spɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摈弃，拒绝</td><td>spurn an offer<br>断然拒绝提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spurned the generous offer without giving any explanation at all.  她断然拒绝了这个优厚的提议，且不作任何解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>embroider</b></td><td>/ɪmˈbrɔɪdər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>（时常伴有夸张和想象内容地）详细说明</td><td>embroider a simple story<br>把简单故事添枝加叶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He embroidered a simple story until nobody could tell what really happened.  他把一个简单的故事添枝加叶，直到没人分得清究竟发生了什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elaborate</td><td>/ɪˈlæbəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>详细阐述；精心制作的</td><td>elaborate on a point<br>详述某一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you elaborate on your last point a little?  你能就最后一点再详细说明一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embellish</td><td>/ɪmˈbelɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，修饰；添枝加叶</td><td>embellish a story<br>给故事添枝加叶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He embellished the tale with details that never actually happened.  他给故事添加了从未发生过的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exaggerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzædʒəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>夸大，夸张</td><td>exaggerate the danger<br>夸大危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tends to exaggerate how long the journey actually takes.  他总爱夸大这段路实际要走多久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overstate</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈsteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>夸大陈述，言过其实</td><td>overstate the benefits<br>夸大好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics say the report overstates the savings from the merger.  批评者说该报告夸大了合并带来的节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 hyperbolize</del></td><td>/haɪˈpɜrbəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>夸张，用夸张手法说</td><td>hyperbolize his own role<br>夸大他自己的作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Advertisers hyperbolize the effects of nearly every new product.  广告商几乎把每种新产品的效果都说得夸张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downplay</td><td>/ˌdaʊnˈpleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写，不予重视</td><td>downplay the significance<br>淡化其重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He downplayed his own role in the company's success.  他淡化了自己在公司成功中的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 de-emphasize</td><td>/diˈɛmfəsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不再强调，减轻重视</td><td>de-emphasize test scores<br>淡化考试分数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy de-emphasizes test scores in admissions decisions.  新政策在录取决定中不再强调考试分数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>emigrate</b></td><td>/ˈemɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>移民，移居海外</td><td>emigrate to Canada<br>移居加拿大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandparents emigrated to Canada during the hard winter after the war.  她的祖父母在战后那个严冬移居加拿大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 migrate</td><td>/ˈmaɪˌɡreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迁移，迁徙</td><td>migrate south for winter<br>南迁过冬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These geese migrate thousands of miles every autumn.  这些大雁每年秋天迁徙数千英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immigrate</td><td>/ˈɪməˌɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>移入，迁入定居</td><td>immigrate to the United States<br>移居美国</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandparents immigrated to this country after the war.  她的祖父母在战后移居到这个国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>eminent</b></td><td>/ˈemɪnənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的</td><td>an eminent legal scholar<br>杰出的法学学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An eminent legal scholar was asked to review the draft constitution.  一位杰出的法学学者受邀审阅宪法草案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 famous</td><td>/ˈfeɪməs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，出名的</td><td>famous for its seafood<br>以海鲜闻名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town is famous for its handmade paper.  这座小镇以手工纸而闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 celebrated</td><td>/ˈseləˌbreɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，受赞誉的</td><td>a celebrated stage actor<br>著名的舞台演员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays work by several celebrated local painters.  博物馆展出了几位著名本地画家的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguished</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卓越的，杰出的；高贵的</td><td>a distinguished scholar<br>杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize went to a distinguished historian of medieval law.  奖项授予一位杰出的中世纪法律史学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 famed</td><td>/ˈfeɪmd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闻名的，著名的</td><td>famed for her courage<br>以勇气闻名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley is famed for its cold, clear spring water.  这座山谷以清冷的泉水而闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 notable</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显著的；值得注意的</td><td>a notable achievement<br>显著的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has been a notable drop in traffic accidents this year.  今年交通事故有显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prominent</td><td>/ˈprɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的；显眼的</td><td>a prominent local politician<br>当地知名的政治人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name appears in a prominent place on the donor list.  她的名字出现在捐赠名单的显眼位置。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renowned</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有名望的，著名的</td><td>renowned throughout Europe<br>闻名整个欧洲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city is renowned for its ancient bridges and narrow lanes.  这座城市以古桥和窄巷闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mediocre</td><td>/ˌmiːdiˈoʊkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸的；质量中等偏下的</td><td>a mediocre performance<br>平庸的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra's mediocre performance disappointed listeners who had waited months for the concert.  乐团平庸的表演让等了几个月的听众很失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undistinguished</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平凡的，不出众的</td><td>an undistinguished career<br>平凡无奇的职业生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had a long but undistinguished career in local government.  他在地方政府中干了很久，却毫不出众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>emollient</b></td><td>/iˈmɑːliənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>起缓和作用的</td><td>an emollient tone of voice<br>缓和气氛的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered in an emollient tone of voice that cooled the argument.  他用缓和的语气回答，让争吵冷了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appeasing</td><td>/əˈpiːzɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>抚慰的，安抚的</td><td>an appeasing gesture<br>安抚性的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised both hands in an appeasing manner and spoke softly.  他举起双手作出安抚的姿态，轻声说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuaging</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缓和的，减轻的</td><td>assuaging words of comfort<br>缓和情绪的安慰话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her assuaging remarks slowly calmed the angry crowd.  她缓和气氛的话慢慢平息了愤怒的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mollifying</del></td><td>/ˈmɑləfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安抚的，使平静的</td><td>a mollifying reply<br>安抚性的回复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager sent a mollifying email to every unhappy client.  经理给每位不满的客户发了安抚性的邮件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mitigating</td><td>/ˈmɪtəˌɡeɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>减轻的，缓解的</td><td>mitigating circumstances<br>可减轻罪责的情形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge considered several mitigating factors before sentencing.  法官在判决前考虑了几项减轻情节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relieving</td><td>/rɪˈliːvɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缓解的，使宽慰的</td><td>a relieving cool breeze<br>令人舒缓的凉风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The relieving news arrived just before the family gave up hope.  这个令人宽慰的消息在家人放弃希望前刚好传来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravating</td><td>/ˈæɡrəˌveɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>使加重的；令人恼火的</td><td>an aggravating delay<br>令人恼火的延误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust is an aggravating factor for people with asthma.  灰尘是哮喘患者病情加重的因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensifying</td><td>/ɪnˈtensəfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>加剧的，增强的</td><td>intensifying political pressure<br>不断加剧的政治压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers face intensifying competition from cheap imports.  农民面临来自廉价进口品日益加剧的竞争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>emulate</b></td><td>/ˈemjuleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>效仿</td><td>emulate his father's style<br>效仿他父亲的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young painter tried to emulate his father's style in every canvas.  这位年轻画家在每幅画布上都力图效仿他父亲的风格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copy</td><td>/ˈkɑːpi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品，仿制品；抄写</td><td>a cheap copy<br>廉价仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting on the wall is only a cheap copy.  墙上那幅画只是廉价的仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imitate</td><td>/ˈɪməˌteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>模仿，仿效</td><td>imitate his accent<br>模仿他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children naturally imitate the speech of adults around them.  孩子会自然地模仿身边大人的说话方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mimic</td><td>/ˈmɪmɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>模仿，仿效；仿造的</td><td>mimic human speech<br>模仿人类说话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain parrots can mimic human speech well enough to fool a casual listener.  某些鹦鹉能模仿人类说话，足以骗过不留意的听者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mime</td><td>/maɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>模仿</td><td>mime the act of drinking<br>模仿喝水的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor mimed the act of drinking tea so convincingly that everyone laughed.  演员模仿喝茶的动作逼真到让所有人都笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>enact</b></td><td>/ɪˈnækt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制定或颁布（法律）</td><td>enact a new tax law<br>制定新税法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament moved quickly to enact a new tax law before the recess.  议会在休会前迅速制定了一部新税法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitute</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，设立；构成</td><td>constitute a committee<br>设立委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board was properly constituted under the new rules.  依据新规则，董事会得以正式组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 establish</td><td>/ɪˈstæblɪʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>确证，证实；建立</td><td>establish the facts beyond doubt<br>确证事实无疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The investigation established the facts beyond any reasonable doubt.  调查毫无疑问地确证了事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 legislate</td><td>/ˈledʒɪˌsleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>立法，制定法律</td><td>legislate against discrimination<br>立法禁止歧视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament moved quickly to legislate on data privacy.  议会迅速就数据隐私进行立法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pass</td><td>/ˈpæs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>通过（法案）；经过</td><td>pass a controversial bill<br>通过一项有争议的法案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate passed the budget after two nights of debate.  经过两晚辩论，参议院通过了预算案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ratify</td><td>/ˈrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（官方地）认可，批准</td><td>ratify the peace treaty<br>批准和平条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate voted to ratify the peace treaty after weeks of debate.  经过数周辩论，参议院投票批准了和平条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ordain</td><td>/ɔːrˈdeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>命令，规定；任命神职</td><td>ordain that all must attend<br>规定所有人必须出席</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old charter ordained that meetings be held every spring.  旧宪章规定会议须于每年春季举行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lay down</td><td>/ˈleɪ ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>制定，规定</td><td>lay down strict conditions<br>规定严格的条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agreement lays down clear rules for sharing the water.  该协议为分配水源制定了明确规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abolish</td><td>/əˈbɑːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>abolish the death penalty<br>废除死刑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government promised to abolish the unpopular entrance fee.  政府承诺废除那项不受欢迎的入场费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repeal</td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rescind</td><td>/rɪˈsɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>rescind an offer<br>撤回报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company rescinded the job offer without explanation.  公司未加解释便撤回了聘用通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revoke</td><td>/rɪˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；吊销</td><td>revoke a license<br>吊销执照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court revoked his driving license after the second offense.  法院在他第二次违法后吊销了他的驾照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>enamel</b></td><td>/ɪˈnæml/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>在…上涂珐琅，上釉</td><td>enamel a copper brooch<br>在铜胸针上施珐琅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Craftsmen enamel each small plate by hand before firing it.  工匠在烧制前用手为每块小片施上珐琅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adorn</td><td>/əˈdɔːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，点缀</td><td>adorn the walls<br>装饰墙面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps and photographs adorn the walls of the study.  旧地图和照片装饰着书房的墙壁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beautify</td><td>/ˈbjuːtɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>美化，装饰</td><td>beautify the city<br>美化城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers planted trees to beautify the neglected riverside park.  志愿者种树来美化那座荒废的河滨公园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bedeck</del></td><td>/bɪˈdek/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，点缀</td><td>bedeck the hall with flowers<br>用花装饰大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers bedeck the hall with flowers before every summer festival.  每年夏季节庆前，村民都用花装饰大厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decorate</td><td>/ˈdekəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，布置；授勋</td><td>decorate the room<br>装饰房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children helped decorate the room with paper lanterns.  孩子们帮忙用纸灯笼装饰房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embellish</td><td>/ɪmˈbelɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，修饰；添枝加叶</td><td>embellish a story<br>给故事添枝加叶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He embellished the tale with details that never actually happened.  他给故事添加了从未发生过的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garnish</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装饰，点缀（食物）</td><td>garnish with fresh herbs<br>用新鲜香草点缀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef garnished each plate with parsley and thin lemon slices.  厨师用欧芹和薄柠檬片点缀每个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ornament</td><td>/ˈɔːrnəˌment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装饰；装饰品</td><td>ornament the hall with flowers<br>用花装饰大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Carved birds ornament the wooden beams of the old temple.  雕刻的飞鸟装饰着古庙的木梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disfigure</td><td>/dɪsˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损毁外形，使难看</td><td>disfigure the facade<br>损毁建筑正面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap repairs disfigured the elegant facade of the old theater.  廉价的修补损毁了老剧院优雅的正面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>encomium</b></td><td>/enˈkoʊmiəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞颂之词</td><td>deliver a warm encomium<br>致以热情的赞颂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor delivered a warm encomium at the retiring teacher's farewell dinner.  市长在退休教师的告别晚宴上致以热情的赞颂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applause</td><td>/əˈplɔːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>鼓掌；认可，赞许</td><td>a round of applause<br>一阵掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech was greeted with long and warm applause.  她的演讲赢得了长时间热烈的掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliment</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞；恭维</td><td>pay someone a compliment<br>赞美某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He paid her a warm compliment on her new painting.  他对她的新画作说了一句诚挚的赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eulogy</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悼词；颂词，赞辞</td><td>deliver a moving eulogy<br>致感人的悼词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delivered a moving eulogy at her grandfather's funeral.  她在祖父的葬礼上致了感人的悼词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 panegyric</td><td>/ˌpænəˈdʒɪrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞颂之词，颂文</td><td>deliver a long panegyric<br>发表一篇长颂词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The retirement dinner ended with a long panegyric to the old professor.  退休晚宴以对这位老教授的长篇颂词结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salutation</td><td>/ˌsæljuˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>致敬，问候</td><td>a salutation to the winners<br>向获胜者致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd offered a warm salutation to the returning champions.  人群向归来的冠军们热情致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tribute</td><td>/ˈtrɪbjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>称颂，颂词</td><td>pay tribute to her work<br>对她的工作表示称颂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Colleagues paid warm tribute to her forty years of patient teaching.  同事们对她四十年的耐心教学致以热情的颂扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 criticism</td><td>/ˈkrɪtɪˌsɪzəm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>批评，指责；评论</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan drew harsh criticism from residents at the meeting.  该计划在会上受到居民的严厉批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>encompass</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，涵盖；包围</td><td>encompass every major topic<br>涵盖所有主要话题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single volume can hardly encompass every major topic in modern physics.  一卷书很难涵盖现代物理学的所有主要话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contain</td><td>/kənˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>控制，抑制；包含</td><td>contain the outbreak<br>控制疫情爆发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors worked around the clock to contain the outbreak.  医生们昼夜不停地努力控制疫情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comprehend</td><td>/ˌkɑːmprɪˈhend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>理解，了解；包括，包含</td><td>comprehend the full report<br>理解整份报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few readers can comprehend the full report without expert help.  很少有读者能在没有专家帮助时理解整份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embody</td><td>/ɪmˈbɑːdi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>体现，包含</td><td>embody the national spirit<br>体现民族精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law embodies years of careful negotiation.  这部新法体现了多年的谨慎谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entail</td><td>/ɪnˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>牵涉，导致</td><td>entail considerable risk<br>牵涉相当大的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebuilding the bridge would entail considerable risk to the workers below.  重建这座桥会给下方的工人带来相当大的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involve</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>涉及，包含；使参与</td><td>involve considerable extra work<br>涉及大量额外工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan involves moving three departments to another building.  这个计划涉及把三个部门迁到另一栋楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subsume</td><td>/səbˈsum/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，归入</td><td>subsume it under one heading<br>把它归入一个标题下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smaller agency was subsumed into the national ministry.  这家小机构被并入了国家部门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exclude</td><td>/ɪkˈskluːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排除，不包括</td><td>exclude him from the list<br>把他从名单中排除</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price excludes taxes and delivery to remote areas.  该价格不包括税费和偏远地区的运费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>encumber</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retard</td><td>/rɪˈtɑːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减速，延迟</td><td>retard the growth<br>延缓生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Language barriers retarded the progress of the peace negotiations.  语言障碍阻碍了和平谈判的进展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stymie</td><td>/ˈstaɪmi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>stymie an investigation<br>阻碍调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing records stymied the investigation for months.  档案缺失使调查停滞了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 promote</td><td>/prəˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，助长；晋升</td><td>promote economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign aims to promote healthy eating among children.  该活动旨在促进儿童健康饮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 further</td><td>/ˈfɜːrðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促进，推动；更远的</td><td>further his career<br>促进他的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took the job abroad to further her research on glaciers.  她接受海外工作以推进她的冰川研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>endemic</b></td><td>/enˈdemɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>地方性的</td><td>endemic to this island<br>这个岛特有的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This flightless bird is endemic to a single island in the Pacific.  这种不会飞的鸟只见于太平洋上的一个岛屿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aboriginal</td><td>/ˌæbəˈrɪdʒənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>原住的，土著的</td><td>aboriginal languages of Taiwan<br>台湾的原住民语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers recorded songs from several aboriginal communities.  研究者录下了几个原住民社区的歌谣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indigenous</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪdʒənəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>土产的，本地的；与生俱来的，先天的</td><td>indigenous plants of the region<br>该地区的本土植物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many indigenous species of the island cannot survive anywhere else on the planet.  这个岛上许多本土物种无法在地球其他任何地方存活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 native</td><td>/ˈneɪtɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本地的，原产的；本地人</td><td>native to southern China<br>原产于华南</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This bamboo is native to the wet valleys of the south.  这种竹子原产于南方潮湿的山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 domestic</td><td>/dəˈmestɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>国内的；家庭的</td><td>the domestic market<br>国内市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the factory's output is sold on the domestic market.  这家工厂的产品大多在国内市场销售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exotic</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːtɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，不同寻常的</td><td>exotic tropical fruit<br>外来的热带水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market sells exotic tropical fruit flown in each morning.  这个市场出售每天清晨空运来的外来热带水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foreign</td><td>/ˈfɔːrən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，异质的；外国的</td><td>a foreign object<br>异物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon removed a foreign object from the patient's eye.  外科医生从病人眼中取出一个异物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonindigenous</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnɪnˈdɪdʒənəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非本土的，外来的</td><td>nonindigenous fish species<br>非本土鱼类物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nonindigenous weeds now cover much of the riverbank.  外来杂草如今覆盖了大片河岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nonnative</td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈneɪtɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非本地的，外来的</td><td>nonnative speakers of English<br>英语非母语者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The park removed several nonnative trees planted in the 1960s.  公园移除了几棵上世纪六十年代种下的外来树木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>endorse</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advocate</td><td>/ˈædvəkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，提倡</td><td>advocate reform<br>提倡改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many doctors advocate walking as the simplest form of exercise.  许多医生提倡把走路作为最简单的运动方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 approve</td><td>/əˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>批准；赞成</td><td>approve the budget<br>批准预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee finally approved the plan after three long meetings.  委员会经过三次长会终于批准了该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certify</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明，认证</td><td>certify the results as valid<br>证明结果有效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An independent lab must certify that the water is safe.  必须由独立实验室证明水质安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 champion</td><td>/ˈtʃæmpiən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持</td><td>champion a cause<br>支持一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has championed the rights of migrant workers for years.  她多年来一直为农民工的权益奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uphold</td><td>/ʌpˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，赞成；维持（原判）</td><td>uphold a decision<br>维持原判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeals court chose to uphold the original ruling in full.  上诉法院决定完全维持原判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deprecate</td><td>/ˈdeprəkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成，反对；贬低</td><td>deprecate his efforts<br>贬低他的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tends to deprecate his own achievements whenever anyone praises them.  每当有人称赞，他总倾向于贬低自己的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impugn</td><td>/ɪmˈpjuːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，质疑（诚信、动机）</td><td>impugn someone's motives<br>质疑某人的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never impugned the honesty of his opponents.  他从不质疑对手的诚实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 oppose</td><td>/əˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反对，抵制</td><td>oppose the new law<br>反对新法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two senators openly opposed the new tax bill.  两位参议员公开反对这项新税案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>enervate</b></del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disable</td><td>/dɪˈseɪbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去能力；停用</td><td>disable the alarm system<br>停用报警系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The injury disabled him for most of the season.  这次受伤让他大半个赛季无法上场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fatigue</td><td>/fəˈtiːɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>疲惫</td><td>suffer from constant fatigue<br>长期感到疲惫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters battling the blaze all day suffered from severe fatigue.  整天与大火搏斗的消防员疲劳不堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sap</td><td>/sæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，逐渐耗尽</td><td>sap his strength<br>削弱他的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of night shifts sapped the energy of the young doctor completely.  数周的夜班彻底削弱了这位年轻医生的精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>enfranchise</b></td><td>/ɪnˈfræntʃaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给予…权利（例如选举权）；解放</td><td>enfranchise women<br>赋予妇女选举权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The amendment enfranchised millions of citizens for the first time.  该修正案首次赋予数百万公民选举权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emancipate</td><td>/ɪˈmænsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，解除束缚</td><td>emancipate the slaves<br>解放奴隶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law emancipated the slaves working on the northern plantations.  新法律解放了在北方种植园劳作的奴隶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 free</td><td>/ˈfriː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，解放；自由的</td><td>free the hostages<br>释放人质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Negotiators finally persuaded the gunmen to free the hostages.  谈判者最终说服枪手释放了人质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liberate</td><td>/ˈlɪbəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，释放</td><td>liberate the city<br>解放这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Allied troops liberated the city in the spring of that year.  盟军在那年春天解放了这座城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 manumit</del></td><td>/ˌmænjuˈmɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放（奴隶）</td><td>manumit his slaves<br>解放他的奴隶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dying planter chose to manumit every slave who had worked his fields.  这位垂死的种植园主决定解放所有为他耕作过的奴隶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rescue</td><td>/ˈreskju/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>解救，营救</td><td>rescue the trapped miners<br>营救被困矿工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helicopters rescued twelve people from the flooded village.  直升机从被淹的村庄救出十二人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unfetter</del></td><td>/ʌnˈfɛtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解除束缚，使自由</td><td>unfetter the market<br>解除对市场的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform unfettered small firms and let them compete freely.  改革解除了对小企业的束缚，让它们自由竞争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disenfranchise</td><td>/dɪsɪnˈfrænˌtʃaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥夺（选举）权利</td><td>disenfranchise poor voters<br>剥夺贫困选民的投票权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics argue the new rules disenfranchise thousands of rural residents.  批评者认为新规剥夺了数千农村居民的投票权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disempower</td><td>/ˌdɪsɛmˈpaʊər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥夺权力，使无力</td><td>disempower local communities<br>剥夺地方社区的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Centralized budgeting disempowered the small district councils.  集中化的预算安排剥夺了小区议会的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bind</td><td>/ˈbaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>捆绑，束缚；约束</td><td>bind his hands<br>捆住他的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty binds both countries to reduce their emissions.  该条约约束两国削减排放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confine</td><td>/kənˈfaɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制</td><td>confine the discussion<br>限定讨论范围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please confine the discussion to the budget for this year.  请把讨论限定在今年的预算上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enfetter</del></td><td>/ɛnˈfɛtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚，使受奴役</td><td>enfetter the human spirit<br>束缚人的精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old superstitions still enfetter the minds of many villagers.  旧迷信仍束缚着许多村民的思想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>engender</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdʒendər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引起，使发展</td><td>engender trust<br>产生信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small acts of honesty engender trust more surely than grand promises do.  小小的诚实之举比宏大的承诺更能可靠地产生信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beget</td><td>/bɪˈɡet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引起，产生；生育</td><td>violence begets violence<br>暴力引发暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless words often beget quarrels that last for years.  轻率的言语常引发持续多年的争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cause</td><td>/ˈkɔːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>原因；导致，引起</td><td>the root cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain caused the small stream to flood the road.  大雨使这条小溪淹了公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catalyze</td><td>/ˈkætəlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>成为…的导火索，导致</td><td>catalyze reform<br>推动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawsuit catalyzed a wave of reform across the industry.  这场诉讼引发了整个行业的改革浪潮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 generate</td><td>/ˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>产生，引起；发电</td><td>generate interest<br>引起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trailer generated enormous interest weeks before the release.  预告片在上映数周前就引起了极大兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 induce</td><td>/ɪnˈduːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>引发，导致</td><td>induce a sense of calm<br>引发平静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soft music and dim lighting induced a deep sense of calm in everyone.  轻柔的音乐和昏暗的灯光让每个人都产生了深深的平静感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invoke</td><td>/ɪnˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>援引；引起，造成</td><td>invoke widespread anger<br>引发广泛的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clumsy apology only invoked more anger among the injured workers.  拙劣的道歉只是在受伤的工人中引发了更多愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 produce</td><td>/prəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>生产，制造；产品</td><td>produce steel locally<br>本地生产钢材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory can produce ten thousand units of steel every month.  这家工厂每月能生产一万单位钢材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spawn</td><td>/spɔːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>引发，大量产生；产卵</td><td>spawn imitations<br>引出大量仿作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hit film spawned three sequels and countless imitations.  这部热门影片催生了三部续集和无数仿作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>engross</b></td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使全神贯注</td><td>engross the young reader<br>使小读者全神贯注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The adventure novel engrossed the young reader for an entire rainy weekend.  这本冒险小说让小读者整个雨天的周末都全神贯注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absorb</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使全神贯注；吸收</td><td>be absorbed in the book<br>沉浸在书里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puzzle absorbed him completely for the whole afternoon.  这道谜题让他整个下午都全神贯注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
