<tr><td>↳近 engage</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>雇用，聘请；从事，参与</td><td>engage a lawyer<br>聘请一位律师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They engaged a local guide to lead them through the forest.  他们聘请了一位当地导游带他们穿过森林。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthrall</td><td>/ɪnˈθrɔːl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（像用魔咒般）吸引</td><td>enthrall the entire audience<br>吸引住全场观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magician enthralled the entire audience with a single deck of cards.  魔术师仅用一副牌就吸引住了全场观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fascinate</td><td>/ˈfæsɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈吸引，使入迷</td><td>fascinate young readers<br>使小读者着迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stories about strange deep-sea creatures fascinate young readers all over the world.  关于奇异深海生物的故事让全世界的小读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grip</td><td>/ˈɡrɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引住；紧握</td><td>grip the reader's attention<br>抓住读者注意力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film gripped us from the very first scene.  这部电影从第一个镜头就抓住了我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immerse</td><td>/ɪˈmɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沉浸；浸入</td><td>immerse herself in research<br>使自己沉浸于研究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He immersed himself in the language by living with a local family.  他住在当地人家中，让自己沉浸在语言环境里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>enigma</b></td><td>/ɪˈnɪɡmə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>难以理解或解释的事物，谜</td><td>remain an enigma<br>仍是个谜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sudden disappearance that autumn remains an enigma to local historians.  他那年秋天的突然失踪对当地史学家来说仍是个谜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mystery</td><td>/ˈmɪstəri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谜，神秘事物</td><td>solve the mystery<br>解开谜团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Detectives worked for years before they solved the mystery.  侦探们工作数年才解开这个谜团。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conundrum</td><td>/kəˈnʌndrəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>难解的问题，谜</td><td>face a difficult conundrum<br>面对棘手难题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 How to feed the city without more farmland is a real conundrum.  如何在不增加农田的情况下养活这座城市是个真正的难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 puzzle</td><td>/ˈpʌzəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使困惑；谜题</td><td>puzzle the doctors<br>使医生们困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sudden recovery puzzled the doctors who had treated her.  她的突然康复让治疗过她的医生们感到困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 riddle</td><td>/ˈrɪdəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>谜语，难题</td><td>answer a riddle<br>解答谜语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man asked a riddle that nobody in the village could answer.  老人问了一个村里没人能答的谜语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>enlighten</b></td><td>/ɪnˈlaɪtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使知道，启发</td><td>enlighten us on the issue<br>就此问题为我们解惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Perhaps you can enlighten us on the issue of the missing receipts.  或许你能就收据丢失的问题为我们解惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprise</td><td>/əˈpraɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>通知，告知</td><td>apprise him of the risks<br>告知他风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please apprise him of the risks before he signs the contract.  请在他签合同之前告知他风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instruct</td><td>/ɪnˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>教导，指导；指示</td><td>instruct new employees<br>指导新员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She instructed the class in basic first aid last week.  上周她教这个班基本的急救知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bewilder</td><td>/bɪˈwɪldər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使迷惑，使不知所措</td><td>bewilder the newcomers<br>使新来者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maze of corridors bewildered every visitor on the first day.  迷宫般的走廊让每位来访者第一天都不知所措。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confuse</td><td>/kənˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；混淆</td><td>confuse the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vague instructions confused nearly everyone in the training class.  含糊的说明让培训班几乎所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confound</td><td>/kənˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；证明为假，证伪</td><td>confound the critics<br>让批评者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The late victory confounded the critics who had predicted failure.  最后的胜利让预言失败的批评者困惑不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perplex</td><td>/pərˈpleks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使费解</td><td>perplex the researchers<br>使研究者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange results perplexed the researchers for several long months.  奇怪的结果让研究者困惑了好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>enmity</b></td><td>/ˈenməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，仇恨</td><td>long-standing enmity<br>积怨已久的仇恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old enmity between the families still poisons village life.  两家的旧仇至今仍毒害着村里的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animosity</td><td>/ˌænəˈmɑːsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，憎恶</td><td>deep animosity between them<br>他们之间的深仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of competition bred real animosity between the two families.  多年的竞争在两家之间催生了真正的敌意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animus</td><td>/ˈænɪməs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意</td><td>personal animus against him<br>对他的个人敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His criticism seemed driven by personal animus rather than honest doubt.  他的批评似乎出于个人敌意，而非真诚的质疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antagonism</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>敌对，对抗</td><td>open antagonism toward reform<br>对改革的公开敌视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open antagonism between the manager and his staff.  经理与员工之间存在公开的敌对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antipathy</td><td>/ænˈtɪpəθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>mutual antipathy<br>相互反感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has long been an antipathy between the two departments.  这两个部门之间长期存在反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feud</td><td>/ˈfjuːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>长期争斗，宿怨</td><td>a bitter family feud<br>激烈的家族宿怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two villages carried on a feud over water rights for decades.  两个村子为水权争斗了几十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gall</td><td>/ɡɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（使）焦躁，激怒</td><td>galled by the remark<br>被那句话惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug tone galled everyone sitting around the conference table.  他自鸣得意的语气激怒了围坐在会议桌旁的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hostility</td><td>/hɑːˈstɪləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，敌对</td><td>open hostility toward<br>对…公开的敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan met with open hostility from local residents.  该计划遭到当地居民的公开敌视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rancor</td><td>/ˈræŋkər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，深仇</td><td>speak without rancor<br>不带怨恨地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the long dispute without any rancor toward her opponents.  她讲述这场长期争端时对对手毫无怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amity</td><td>/ˈæməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友好关系，亲善和睦</td><td>a treaty of amity<br>友好条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a treaty of amity and commerce.  两国签署了友好通商条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comity</td><td>/ˈkɑːmɪti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友好，礼让；和睦</td><td>the comity of nations<br>国家间的礼让</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty was signed in a spirit of genuine comity.  条约是在真诚友好的气氛中签署的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ennoble</b></td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 canonize</td><td>/ˈkænənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，封为圣徒</td><td>canonize a saint<br>封为圣徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church voted to canonize the nun a century after her death.  教会在这位修女去世百年后决定将她封为圣徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dignify</td><td>/ˈdɪɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使有尊严，使显庄重</td><td>dignify the ceremony<br>使典礼显得庄重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet presence dignified the otherwise chaotic opening ceremony.  她安静的在场使原本混乱的开幕式显得庄重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debase</td><td>/dɪˈbeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，贬损</td><td>debase the currency<br>使货币贬值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians who lie to win votes debase the office they hold.  为拉票而撒谎的政客贬损了他们所担任的职务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humiliate</td><td>/hjuːˈmɪlieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>羞辱，使丧失尊严</td><td>humiliate him in public<br>当众羞辱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never humiliated a student for asking a simple question.  这位老师从不因学生问简单的问题而羞辱他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ennui</b></td><td>/ɑːnˈwiː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠；缺乏兴趣</td><td>a deep sense of ennui<br>深深的倦怠感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deep sense of ennui settled over the students during the long summer.  漫长的夏天里，一种深深的倦怠感笼罩着学生们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doldrums</td><td>/ˈdoʊldrəmz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>低迷，停滞</td><td>in the doldrums<br>处于低迷状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The housing market has been in the doldrums all year.  房地产市场整年都处于低迷状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tedium</td><td>/ˈtiːdiəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>单调乏味，沉闷</td><td>relieve the tedium of waiting<br>缓解等待的乏味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She read novels to escape the tedium of the long commute.  她读小说以逃避长途通勤的乏味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weariness</td><td>/ˈwɪrinəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疲倦，厌倦</td><td>a growing weariness with politics<br>对政治日增的厌倦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You could hear the weariness in the old teacher's voice.  你能从老教师的声音里听出疲倦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keen interest</td><td>/ˈkiːn ˈɪntrəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>浓厚的兴趣</td><td>take a keen interest<br>怀有浓厚兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed a keen interest in insects from an early age.  她从小就对昆虫表现出浓厚的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 energy</td><td>/ˈenərdʒi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精力，活力；能量</td><td>full of restless energy<br>充满旺盛的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children still had plenty of energy after the long hike.  长途徒步之后孩子们仍精力充沛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enthusiasm</td><td>/ɪnˈθuːziæzəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热情，热忱</td><td>greet the plan with enthusiasm<br>热情地迎接该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enthusiasm for the project convinced the whole committee.  她对这个项目的热情说服了整个委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exuberance</td><td>/ɪɡˈzuːbərəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>热情洋溢，兴高采烈</td><td>youthful exuberance<br>年轻人的热情洋溢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band played with an exuberance that lifted the whole room.  乐队演奏得热情洋溢，带动了满屋气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>expostulate</b></del></td><td>/ɪkˈspɑːstʃuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抗议，规劝</td><td>expostulate with the officials<br>向官员们提出抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers expostulated with the officials about the new road.  村民们就这条新路向官员们提出抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demur</td><td>/dɪˈmɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>表示异议，反对</td><td>demur at the suggestion<br>对建议表示异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She demurred at the suggestion that she should resign.  对于让她辞职的建议，她表示了异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inveigh</del></td><td>/ɪnˈveɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>猛烈抨击，痛斥</td><td>inveigh against the new tax<br>激烈抗议新税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers gathered downtown to inveigh against the new fuel tax.  农民们聚集在市中心痛斥新的燃油税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accede</td><td>/əkˈsiːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同意，答应；就任</td><td>accede to a request<br>答应请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government finally acceded to the union's demands.  政府最终同意了工会的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agree</td><td>/əˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同意，赞同；一致</td><td>agree to the terms<br>同意这些条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to the new safety rules.  双方最终同意了新的安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approve</td><td>/əˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>批准；赞成</td><td>approve the budget<br>批准预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee finally approved the plan after three long meetings.  委员会经过三次长会终于批准了该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>ensconce</b></del></td><td>/ɪnˈskɑːns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安置，安顿；隐藏</td><td>ensconce oneself in<br>把自己安置在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She ensconced herself in the armchair nearest the fire and opened her book.  她把自己安置在离火最近的扶手椅里，打开了书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bury</td><td>/ˈberi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>埋葬，掩埋</td><td>bury the treasure<br>埋藏宝藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squirrels bury nuts all over the garden every autumn.  松鼠每年秋天把坚果埋在花园各处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cache</td><td>/kæʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>囤货，藏货</td><td>a cache of weapons<br>一批藏匿的武器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police discovered a cache of weapons beneath the barn floor.  警方在谷仓地板下发现了一批藏匿的武器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secrete</td><td>/sɪˈkriːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隐藏；分泌</td><td>secrete the documents<br>把文件藏起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver had secreted the documents beneath the front seat.  司机把文件藏在了前排座椅下面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 display</td><td>/dɪsˈpleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展示，陈列；显露</td><td>display the paintings<br>展出这些画作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays its rarest coins in a locked glass case.  博物馆把最珍稀的钱币陈列在上锁的玻璃柜里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhibit</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪbɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展出；显示，表现出</td><td>exhibit great courage<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children often exhibit no fear of strangers at all.  幼童常常对陌生人完全不表现出畏惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ensue</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>紧随其后</td><td>the chaos that ensued<br>随之而来的混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bitter argument ensued as soon as the results were read aloud.  结果一被大声读出，激烈的争吵便随之而来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 follow</td><td>/ˈfɑːloʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵循，跟随</td><td>follow the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 If you follow the instructions exactly, nothing can go wrong.  只要严格按说明操作，就不会出错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
